This alternating strategy decomposes joint posterior sampling into simpler subproblems, avoiding gradient conflicts while retaining the complementary strengths of both domains. Experiments on FFHQ demonstrate that the proposed ADDPS achieves superior perceptual quality compared with existing methods.

</details>


### [38] [Frequency-Decomposed INR for NIR-Assisted Low-Light RGB Image Denoising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16800)
*Ligen Shi,Zengyu Pang,Chang Liu,Shuchen Sun,Jun Qiu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于频率解耦隐式神经表示（FD-INR）的近红外辅助低光图像恢复方法，通过多尺度小波变换将图像分解为不同频率分量，利用RGB-NIR跨模态频率相关性统计先验，实现低频颜色和高频纹理的互补恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决低光条件下可见光图像存在的严重噪声和高频结构退化问题。传统方法在空间域进行刚性融合容易导致颜色失真和伪影，需要一种能够充分利用RGB-NIR跨模态频率相关性的恢复方法。

Method: 基于频率解耦隐式神经表示（FD-INR）框架：1）通过多尺度小波变换将图像分解为不同频率分量；2）构建双分支隐式神经表示，利用RGB-NIR跨模态频率相关性统计先验（低频RGB信号更可靠，高频NIR信号相关性更高）；3）设计跨模态差异化频率监督机制，用低光RGB指导低频亮度和颜色重建，用高信噪比NIR信号约束高频纹理细节生成；4）引入基于不确定性的自适应加权损失函数，自动平衡不同频率任务的贡献。

Result: FD-INR不仅有效恢复了图像亮度一致性和结构细节，而且得益于其隐式连续表示特性，在任意分辨率重建任务中优于现有方法，显著提升了低光感知的可靠性。

Conclusion: 提出的FD-INR方法通过频率解耦和跨模态差异化监督，成功解决了低光图像恢复中的噪声和结构退化问题，避免了传统空间域融合方法的颜色失真和伪影，实现了更可靠的图像恢复效果。

Abstract: Addressing the issues of severe noise and high frequency structural degradation in visible images under low-light conditions, this paper proposes a Near Infrared (NIR) aided low light image restoration method based on Frequency Decoupled Implicit Neural Representation (FDINR). Based on the statistical prior of RGB-NIR cross-modal frequency correlations, specifically that low-frequency RGB signals are more reliable, whereas high frequency NIR signals exhibit higher correlation, we explicitly decompose images into distinct frequency components via multi-scale wavelet transforms and construct a dual-branch implicit neural representation framework. Within this framework, we design a cross modal differentiated frequency supervision mechanism, leveraging low light RGB to guide the reconstruction of low frequency luminance and color, and utilizing high-SNR NIR signals to constrain the generation of high frequency texture details, thereby achieving complementary advantages in the frequency domain. Furthermore, an uncertainty-based adaptive weighting loss function is introduced to automatically balance the contributions of different frequency tasks, solving the problems of color distortion and artifacts caused by rigid fusion in the spatial domain common in traditional methods. Experimental results demonstrate that FD-INR not only effectively restores image luminance consistency and structural details but also, benefitting from its implicit continuous representation, outperforms existing methods in arbitrary-resolution reconstruction tasks, significantly enhancing the reliability of low light perception.

</details>


### [39] [Modeling Biomechanical Constraint Violations for Language-Agnostic Lip-Sync Deepfake Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16808)
*Hao Chen,Junnan Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BioLip：基于生物力学约束的唇语同步深度伪造检测器，通过分析唇部时间抖动来识别伪造视频


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有唇语同步深度伪造检测器依赖像素级伪影或音视频对应关系，这些特征在不同语言间泛化能力差，因为它们编码的是数据依赖模式而非通用物理规律。需要找到更基础的检测原理。

Method: 提出BioLip框架，基于生物力学约束原理：生成模型不强制执行真实口面部运动的生物力学约束，导致可测量的时间唇部方差增加（称为时间唇部抖动）。使用MediaPipe提取64个口周关键点坐标，通过分析这些关键点的时间抖动模式进行检测。

Result: 该方法在不同说话者语言、种族和录制条件下具有一致的检测性能，因为时间唇部抖动是生成模型的固有缺陷，不受数据特定特征影响。

Conclusion: 生物力学约束为唇语同步深度伪造检测提供了更基础、更泛化的原理，BioLip框架通过分析时间唇部抖动有效识别伪造视频，且具有轻量级优势。

Abstract: Current lip-sync deepfake detectors rely on pixel-level artifacts or audio-visual correspondence, failing to generalize across languages because these cues encode data-dependent patterns rather than universal physical laws. We identify a more fundamental principle: generative models do not enforce the biomechanical constraints of authentic orofacial articulation, producing measurably elevated temporal lip variance -- a signal we term temporal lip jitter -- that is empirically consistent across the speaker's language, ethnicity, and recording conditions. We instantiate this principle through BioLip, a lightweight framework operating on 64 perioral landmark coordinates extracted by MediaPipe.

</details>


### [40] [Lorentz Framework for Semantic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16836)
*Zahid Hasan,Masud Ahmed,Nirmalya Roy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基于洛伦兹模型的语义分割框架，利用双曲空间建模层次结构，提供不确定性量化，无需黎曼优化器，可集成现有欧几里得架构


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于庞加莱球模型的双曲语义分割方法存在数值不稳定、优化困难和计算挑战等问题，需要更稳定高效的解决方案

Method: 提出基于洛伦兹模型的双曲语义分割框架，利用文本嵌入的语义和视觉线索指导洛伦兹空间中的层次像素级表示，无需黎曼优化器，可轻松集成现有欧几里得架构

Result: 在ADE20K、COCO-Stuff-164k、Pascal-VOC和Cityscapes数据集上验证了方法的有效性，使用DeepLabV3、SegFormer、mask2former和maskformer等模型，实现了稳定优化、不确定性估计、置信度图、边界描绘、层次检索和零样本性能

Conclusion: 洛伦兹双曲嵌入为鲁棒且具有不确定性感知的语义分割提供了潜力，解决了庞加莱模型的局限性，提供了更稳定高效的解决方案

Abstract: Semantic segmentation in hyperbolic space enables compact modeling of hierarchical structure while providing inherent uncertainty quantification. Prior approaches predominantly rely on the Poincaré ball model, which suffers from numerical instability, optimization, and computational challenges. We propose a novel, tractable, architecture-agnostic semantic segmentation framework (pixel-wise and mask classification) in the hyperbolic Lorentz model. We employ text embeddings with semantic and visual cues to guide hierarchical pixel-level representations in Lorentz space. This enables stable and efficient optimization without requiring a Riemannian optimizer, and easily integrates with existing Euclidean architectures. Beyond segmentation, our approach yields free uncertainty estimation, confidence map, boundary delineation, hierarchical and text-based retrieval, and zero-shot performance, reaching generalized flatter minima. We introduce a novel uncertainty and confidence indicator in Lorentz cone embeddings. Further, we provide analytical and empirical insights into Lorentz optimization via gradient analysis. Extensive experiments on ADE20K, COCO-Stuff-164k, Pascal-VOC, and Cityscapes, utilizing state-of-the-art per-pixel classification models (DeepLabV3 and SegFormer) and mask classification models (mask2former and maskformer), validate the effectiveness and generality of our approach. Our results demonstrate the potential of hyperbolic Lorentz embeddings for robust and uncertainty-aware semantic segmentation. Code is available at https://github.com/mxahan/Lorentz_semantic_segmentation.

</details>


### [41] [TowerDataset: A Heterogeneous Benchmark for Transmission Corridor Segmentation with a Global-Local Fusion Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16848)
*Xu Cui,Xinyan Liu,Chen Yang,Zhaobo Qi,Beichen Zang,Weigang Zhang,Antoni B. Chan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了TowerDataset传输走廊点云细粒度分割基准数据集和全局-局部融合框架，解决现有数据稀缺、场景短、类别粗的问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有传输走廊点云分割面临现实数据稀缺、难以建模长距离异构场景的全局结构和局部几何细节、现有公开数据集类别粗且场景短、缺乏长距离结构依赖和严重长尾分布等问题

Method: 1) 提出TowerDataset包含661个真实场景约24.66亿点，保留长走廊范围、定义22类细粒度分类体系；2) 提出全局-局部融合框架：全局分支采用NoCrop训练和原型对比学习捕获长距离拓扑和上下文依赖，局部分支保留精细几何结构，通过几何验证融合和精化预测

Result: 在TowerDataset和两个公开基准上的实验证明了所提基准的挑战性以及框架在真实、复杂、异构传输走廊场景中的鲁棒性

Conclusion: TowerDataset填补了传输走廊点云细粒度分割的基准空白，提出的全局-局部融合框架能有效利用全局关系和局部形状细节识别稀有和易混淆组件

Abstract: Fine-grained semantic segmentation of transmission-corridor point clouds is fundamental for intelligent power-line inspection. However, current progress is limited by realistic data scarcity and the difficulty of modeling global corridor structure and local geometric details in long, heterogeneous scenes. Existing public datasets usually provide only a few coarse categories or short cropped scenes which overlook long-range structural dependencies, severe long-tail distributions, and subtle distinctions among safety-critical components. As a result, current methods are difficult to evaluate under realistic inspection settings, and their ability to preserve and integrate complementary global and local cues remains unclear.
  To address the above challenges, we introduce TowerDataset, a heterogeneous benchmark for transmission-corridor segmentation. TowerDataset contains 661 real-world scenes and about 2.466 billion points. It preserves long corridor extents, defines a fine-grained 22-class taxonomy, and provides standardized splits and evaluation protocols.
  In addition, we present a global-local fusion framework which preserves and fuses whole-scene and local-detail information. A whole-scene branch with NoCrop training and prototypical contrastive learning captures long-range topology and contextual dependencies. A block-wise local branch retains fine geometric structures. Both predictions are then fused and refined by geometric validation. This design allows the model to exploit both global relationships and local shape details when recognizing rare and confusing components. Experiments on TowerDataset and two public benchmarks demonstrate the challenge of the proposed benchmark and the robustness of our framework in real, complex, and heterogeneous transmission-corridor scenes. The dataset will be released soon at https://huggingface.co/datasets/tccx18/Towerdataset/tree/main.

</details>


### [42] [CATP: Confidence-Aware Token Pruning for Camouflaged Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16854)
*Yuhan Gao,Shuhao Kang,Xin He,Bing Li,Xu Cheng,Yun Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CATP框架，通过分层置信感知令牌剪枝减少Transformer在伪装目标检测中的计算开销，同时保持高精度


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer在伪装目标检测中虽达到SOTA精度，但计算开销大限制了实际部署，需要高效解决方案

Method: 分层置信感知令牌剪枝框架，识别并丢弃易区分的背景和物体内部令牌，聚焦关键边界令牌；引入双路径特征补偿机制聚合剪枝令牌的上下文知识

Result: 在多个COD基准测试中显著降低计算复杂度，同时保持高精度

Conclusion: CATP为COD模型在实际场景中的高效部署提供了有前景的研究方向

Abstract: Camouflaged Object Detection (COD) aims to segment targets that share extreme textural and structural similarities with their complex environments. Leveraging their capacity for long-range dependency modeling, Transformer-based detectors have become the mainstream approach and achieve state-of-the-art (SoTA) accuracy, yet their substantial computational overhead severely limits practical deployment. To address this, we propose a hierarchical Confidence-Aware Token Pruning framework (CATP) tailored for COD. Our approach hierarchically identifies and discards easily distinguishable tokens from both background and object interiors, focusing computations on critical boundary tokens. To compensate for information loss from pruning, we introduce a dual-path feature compensation mechanism that aggregates contextual knowledge from pruned tokens into enriched features. Extensive experiments on multiple COD benchmarks demonstrate that our method significantly reduces computational complexity while maintaining high accuracy, offering a promising research direction for the efficient deployment of COD models in real-world scenarios. The code will be released.

</details>


### [43] [When W4A4 Breaks Camouflaged Object Detection: Token-Group Dual-Constraint Activation Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16855)
*Tianqi Li,Wenyu Fang,Xin He,Xue Geng,Xu Cheng,Yun Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出COD-TDQ方法，针对伪装目标检测任务的4位量化难题，通过令牌分组双约束激活量化解决令牌局部瓶颈问题，在多个基准上显著优于现有量化方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 伪装目标检测需要在有限的内存和延迟预算下进行，因此低比特推理具有重要价值。然而，COD任务对量化特别敏感，因为其预测依赖于细微的纹理和边界线索。研究发现，Transformer-based COD在W4A4量化中存在任务特定的"悬崖效应"：重尾背景令牌主导共享激活范围，导致步长膨胀并将弱但有结构的边界线索推入零值箱，形成令牌局部瓶颈。

Method: 提出COD-TDQ方法，包含两个耦合步骤：1) 直接和令牌分组：为令牌组分配尺度以抑制跨令牌范围主导；2) 双约束范围投影：投影每个令牌组的裁剪范围，保持步长-离散度比和零值箱质量有界。该方法专门针对COD任务的量化特性设计，无需重新训练。

Result: 在四个COD基准和两个基线模型（CFRN和ESCNet）上，COD-TDQ始终比最先进的量化方法获得超过0.12的Sα分数提升，显著改善了4位量化下的伪装目标检测性能。

Conclusion: COD-TDQ通过解决令牌局部瓶颈问题，成功实现了Transformer-based伪装目标检测的高效4位量化，为资源受限环境下的COD部署提供了有效的解决方案。该方法展示了任务感知量化策略的重要性，特别是在处理具有特殊激活分布的任务时。

Abstract: Camouflaged object detection (COD) segments objects that intentionally blend with the background, so predictions depend on subtle texture and boundary cues. COD is often needed under tight on-device memory and latency budgets, making low-bit inference highly desirable. However, COD is unusually hard to quantify aggressively. We study post-training W4A4 quantization of Transformer-based COD and find a task-specific cliff: heavy-tailed background tokens dominate a shared activation range, inflating the step size and pushing weak-but-structured boundary cues into the zero bin. This exposes a token-local bottleneck -- remove cross-token range domination and bound the zero-bin mass under 4-bit activations. To address this, we introduce COD-TDQ, a COD-aware Token-group Dual-constraint activation Quantization method. COD-TDQ addresses this token-local bottleneck with two coupled steps: Direct-Sum Token-Group (DSTG) assigns token-group scales to suppress cross-token range domination, and Dual-Constraint Range Projection (DCRP) projects each token-group clip range to keep the step-to-dispersion ratio and the zero-bin mass bounded. Across four COD benchmarks and two baseline models (CFRN and ESCNet), COD-TDQ consistently achieves an Sαscore more than 0.12 higher than that of the state-of-the-art quantization method without retraining. The code will be released.

</details>

### [44] [Multimodal Claim Extraction for Fact-Checking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16311)
*Joycelyn Teo,Rui Cao,Zhenyun Deng,Zifeng Ding,Michael Sejr Schlichtkrull,Andreas Vlachos*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 首个多模态社交媒体声明提取基准，评估现有MLLMs表现不佳，提出MICE框架提升意图感知能力


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动事实核查方法主要关注文本声明提取，忽视了当今错误信息的跨模态特性。社交媒体帖子通常结合简短非正式文本与图像（如表情包、截图、照片），这带来了与纯文本声明提取和成熟多模态任务不同的挑战。

Method: 1. 创建首个社交媒体多模态声明提取基准，包含文本和图像的帖子，标注来自真实事实核查者的黄金标准声明。2. 在三部分评估框架（语义对齐、忠实度、去语境化）下评估最先进的多模态大语言模型。3. 提出MICE（意图感知框架）来解决基线MLLMs在建模修辞意图和上下文线索方面的困难。

Result: 基线多模态大语言模型在建模修辞意图和上下文线索方面表现不佳。提出的MICE框架在意图关键案例中显示出改进。

Conclusion: 多模态声明提取是自动事实核查的重要但被忽视的步骤，需要专门的方法来处理社交媒体内容的独特特性。MICE框架通过意图感知方法展示了改进潜力。

Abstract: Automated Fact-Checking (AFC) relies on claim extraction as a first step, yet existing methods largely overlook the multimodal nature of today's misinformation. Social media posts often combine short, informal text with images such as memes, screenshots, and photos, creating challenges that differ from both text-only claim extraction and well-studied multimodal tasks like image captioning or visual question answering. In this work, we present the first benchmark for multimodal claim extraction from social media, consisting of posts containing text and one or more images, annotated with gold-standard claims derived from real-world fact-checkers. We evaluate state-of-the-art multimodal LLMs (MLLMs) under a three-part evaluation framework (semantic alignment, faithfulness, and decontextualization) and find that baseline MLLMs struggle to model rhetorical intent and contextual cues. To address this, we introduce MICE, an intent-aware framework which shows improvements in intent-critical cases.

</details>


### [45] [Cross-Family Speculative Decoding for Polish Language Models on Apple~Silicon: An Empirical Evaluation of Bielik~11B with UAG-Extended MLX-LM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16368)
*Krzysztof Fonal*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究扩展了MLX-LM框架，实现了跨分词器的推测解码，在Apple Silicon上评估了波兰语LLM的跨家族推测解码性能，发现上下文感知分词翻译能提升接受率，但统一内存架构下的验证成本未按理论预期摊销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推测解码通常用于相同分词器的高带宽GPU场景，但跨家族模型（分词器不匹配）在消费级统一内存架构上的适用性尚未充分探索。本研究旨在评估波兰语LLM在Apple Silicon上的跨分词器推测解码性能。

Method: 扩展MLX-LM框架，引入通用辅助生成（UAG）支持跨分词器推测解码。使用Bielik 11B-Instruct（基于Mistral）作为目标模型，搭配三种草稿模型：Bielik 1.5B（基于Qwen的自定义分词器）、Qwen2.5-1.5B和Llama 3.2-1B。在三个波兰语数据集（Wikipedia、pl_alpaca、合成数据）上测试，比较朴素和上下文感知的分词翻译方法，草稿长度k∈{2,4,6}。

Result: 1. 上下文感知分词翻译在所有配置中持续提升接受率；2. 波兰语专用Bielik 1.5B草稿模型比通用Qwen2.5和Llama 3.2接受率更低；3. Apple Silicon上的吞吐量具有内容依赖性，结构化文本可达1.7倍加速，但多样化指令场景加速失败；4. 统一内存上的验证成本未按理论预期摊销，因为两个模型都受内存带宽限制，使得顺序草稿生成相对于批量验证成本较高。

Conclusion: 提出了硬件感知的加速公式，并描述了Apple Silicon上跨家族推测解码的条件。这是首个针对波兰语LLM的跨家族推测解码系统评估，也是首个基于UAG的统一内存架构解码实证研究，为跨分词器推测解码在消费级硬件上的实际应用提供了重要见解。

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates LLM inference by using a small draft model to propose k candidate tokens for a target model to verify. While effective for same-tokenizer pairs on high-bandwidth GPUs, its applicability to cross-family pairs with mismatched tokenizers and consumer-grade unified memory remains underexplored. We extend the MLX-LM framework with Universal Assisted Generation (UAG) to enable cross-tokenizer speculative decoding on Apple Silicon. We evaluate Bielik 11B-Instruct (Mistral-based) as the target model, paired with three draft models: Bielik 1.5B (Qwen-based with custom tokenizer), Qwen2.5-1.5B, and Llama 3.2-1B. Experiments on three Polish-language datasets (Wikipedia, pl_alpaca, synthetic) use draft lengths k in {2, 4, 6} to compare naive and context-aware token translation. Results show: (1) context-aware translation consistently improves acceptance rates across all configurations; (2) the Polish-specialized Bielik 1.5B achieves lower acceptance than general-purpose Qwen2.5 and Llama 3.2 drafters; (3) throughput on Apple Silicon is content-dependent, reaching 1.7x speedup for structured text but failing for varied instructions; and (4) verification cost on unified memory does not amortize as theory predicts because both models are memory-bandwidth bound, making sequential drafting expensive relative to batched verification. We propose a hardware-aware speedup formula and characterize conditions for cross-family speculative decoding on Apple Silicon. This is the first systematic evaluation of cross-family speculative decoding for Polish LLMs and the first empirical study of UAG-based decoding on unified memory architectures.

</details>


### [46] [Brain-CLIPLM: Decoding Compressed Semantic Representations in EEG for Language Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16370)
*Xiaoli Yang,Huiyuan Tian,Yurui Li,Jianyu Zhang,Shijian Li,Gang Pan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Brain-CLIPLM：一种两阶段EEG到文本解码框架，通过语义锚点提取和检索增强的LLM重建，将EEG解码重新定义为语义压缩恢复而非完整句子重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统EEG到文本解码受限于低信噪比和有限信息带宽，完整句子重建可能不切实际。作者提出语义压缩假说：EEG信号编码的是压缩的语义锚点而非完整语言结构，需要更匹配神经信息容量的解码方法。

Method: 提出Brain-CLIPLM两阶段框架：1) 通过对比学习提取EEG中的语义锚点表示；2) 使用检索增强的大型语言模型（LLM）结合思维链（CoT）推理进行句子重建，遵循粒度匹配原则使解码复杂度与神经信息容量对齐。

Result: 在Zurich认知语言处理语料库上，Brain-CLIPLM达到67.55% top-5和85.00% top-25句子检索准确率，显著优于直接解码基线。跨被试评估确认了稳健的泛化能力。控制分析（包括置换测试）表明EEG表示携带超越语言模型先验的句子特定信息。

Conclusion: EEG到文本解码应被重新定义为恢复压缩语义内容而非重建完整句子，这为无创脑机接口提供了生物学基础和更高效的数据利用途径。语义压缩假说为低带宽神经信号的语言解码提供了更现实的框架。

Abstract: Decoding natural language from non-invasive electroencephalography (EEG) remains fundamentally limited by low signal-to-noise ratio and restricted information bandwidth. This raises a fundamental question regarding whether sentence-level linguistic structure can be reliably recovered from such signals. In this work, we suggest that this assumption may not hold under realistic information constraints, and instead propose a semantic compression hypothesis in which EEG signals encode a compressed set of semantic anchors rather than full linguistic structure. Under our new perspective, direct sentence reconstruction becomes an overparameterized objective relative to the intrinsic information capacity of EEG. To address this mismatch, we introduce Brain-CLIPLM, a two-stage framework that decomposes EEG-to-text decoding into semantic anchor extraction via contrastive learning and sentence reconstruction using a retrieval-grounded large language model (LLM) with Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning, following a granularity matching principle that aligns decoding complexity with neural information capacity. Evaluated on the Zurich Cognitive Language Processing Corpus, Brain-CLIPLM achieves 67.55\% top-5 and 85.00\% top-25 sentence retrieval accuracy, significantly outperforming direct decoding baseline, while cross-subject evaluation confirms robust generalization. Control analyses, including permutation testing, further demonstrate that EEG-derived representations carry sentence-specific information beyond language model priors. These results suggest that EEG-to-text decoding is better framed as recovering compressed semantic content rather than reconstructing full sentences, providing a biologically grounded and data-efficient pathway for non-invasive brain-computer interfaces.

</details>


### [47] [CFMS: Towards Explainable and Fine-Grained Chinese Multimodal Sarcasm Detection Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16372)
*Junzhao Zhang,Hsiu-Yuan Huang,Chenming Tang,Yutong Yang,Yunfang Wu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 构建了首个面向中文社交媒体的细粒度多模态讽刺检测数据集CFMS，包含2,796个高质量图文对，提供讽刺识别、目标识别和解释生成三级标注框架，并提出强化学习增强的上下文学习策略PGDS优化示例选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态讽刺检测基准存在标注粒度粗糙和文化覆盖有限的问题，阻碍了细粒度语义理解研究，需要构建更精细的中文社交媒体讽刺数据集。

Method: 构建CFMS数据集，包含2,796个高质量中文社交媒体图文对，采用三级标注框架：讽刺识别、目标识别和解释生成；同时构建中英平行隐喻子集；提出强化学习增强的上下文学习策略PGDS动态优化示例选择。

Result: 细粒度解释标注有效指导AI生成具有明确讽刺意图的图像；当前模型在隐喻推理方面存在显著局限性；PGDS方法在关键任务上显著优于现有基线。

Conclusion: CFMS为构建可靠的多模态讽刺理解系统提供了坚实基础，PGDS方法通过动态优化示例选择显著提升了多模态讽刺检测性能。

Abstract: Multimodal sarcasm detection has recently garnered significant attention. However, existing benchmarks suffer from coarse-grained annotations and limited cultural coverage, which hinder research into fine-grained semantic understanding. To address this, we construct CFMS, the first fine-grained multimodal sarcasm dataset tailored for Chinese social media. It comprises 2,796 high-quality image-text pairs and provides a triple-level annotation framework: sarcasm identification, target recognition, and explanation generation. We find that the fine-grained explanation annotations effectively guide AI in generating images with explicit sarcastic intent. Furthermore, we curate a high-consistency parallel Chinese-English metaphor subset (200 entries each), revealing significant limitations of current models in metaphoric reasoning. To overcome the constraints of traditional retrieval methods, we propose a Reinforcement Learning-augmented In-Context Learning strategy (PGDS) to dynamically optimize exemplar selection. Extensive experiments demonstrate that CFMS provides a solid foundation for building reliable multimodal sarcasm understanding systems, and the PGDS method significantly outperforms existing baselines on key tasks. Our data and code are available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/CFMS-E8F9.

</details>


### [48] [Foundational Study on Authorship Attribution of Japanese Web Reviews for Actor Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16376)
*Hiroshi Matsubara,Shingo Matsugaya,Taichi Aoki,Masaki Hashimoto*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了基于风格特征进行作者归属分析在威胁情报中支持参与者分析的适用性，使用日本乐天市场评论数据进行实验，比较了四种方法，发现BERT微调在小规模作者数时表现最佳，但在作者数量达到数百时TF-IDF+逻辑回归在准确性、稳定性和计算成本方面更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是探索作者归属分析在威胁情报领域的应用潜力，特别是支持参与者分析。作为未来应用于暗网论坛的基础步骤，研究首先在明网数据上进行验证，使用日本评论数据作为实验材料。

Method: 研究方法包括：1）从乐天市场构建评论数据集；2）比较四种作者归属方法：TF-IDF+逻辑回归、BERT嵌入+逻辑回归、BERT微调、度量学习+k近邻；3）进行Top-k评估和错误分析。

Result: 实验结果显示：1）BERT微调在小规模作者数时表现最佳；2）当作者数量扩展到数百时，TF-IDF+逻辑回归在准确性、稳定性和计算成本方面表现更优；3）Top-k评估证明了候选筛选的实用性；4）错误分析发现模板文本、主题依赖性和文本长度短是导致误分类的主要因素。

Conclusion: 研究表明作者归属分析在威胁情报中具有应用潜力，但方法选择需根据作者规模权衡。BERT微调适合小规模作者识别，而TF-IDF+逻辑回归在大规模作者场景下更具优势。未来可将该方法应用于暗网论坛的参与者分析。

Abstract: This study investigates the applicability of authorship attribution based on stylistic features to support actor analysis in threat intelligence. As a foundational step toward future application to dark web forums, we conducted experiments using Japanese review data from clear web sources. We constructed datasets from Rakuten Ichiba reviews and compared four methods: TF-IDF with logistic regression (TF-IDF+LR), BERT embeddings with logistic regression (BERT-Emb+LR), BERT fine-tuning (BERT-FT), and metric learning with $k$-nearest neighbors (Metric+kNN). Results showed that BERT-FT achieved the best performance; however, training became unstable as the number of authors scaled to several hundred, where TF-IDF+LR proved superior in terms of accuracy, stability, and computational cost. Furthermore, Top-$k$ evaluation demonstrated the utility of candidate screening, and error analysis revealed that boilerplate text, topic dependency, and short text length were primary factors causing misclassification.

</details>


### [49] [GoCoMA: Hyperbolic Multimodal Representation Fusion for Large Language Model-Generated Code Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16377)
*Nitin Choudhury,Bikrant Bikram Pratap Maurya,Bhavinkumar Vinodbhai Kuwar,Arun Balaji Buduru*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: GoCoMA是一个多模态框架，通过结合代码风格特征和二进制预执行工件的图像表示，在双曲空间中融合特征来识别代码的LLM来源


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM生成的代码越来越难以与人类代码区分，这引发了安全漏洞、许可模糊等实际问题，需要开发能够准确识别代码来源（哪个LLM生成）的取证方法

Method: GoCoMA采用多模态框架，建模代码风格特征（高层次结构风格签名）和二进制预执行工件图像表示（低层次字节语义）之间的外在层次关系。将模态嵌入投影到双曲庞加莱球中，通过基于测地线余弦相似度的跨模态注意力融合机制融合特征，然后将融合表示反投影到欧几里得空间进行最终LLM来源归属

Result: 在两个开源基准测试（CoDET-M4和LLMAuthorBench）上的实验表明，在相同评估协议下，GoCoMA始终优于单模态和欧几里得多模态基线方法

Conclusion: GoCoMA通过结合代码风格和二进制工件的多模态表示，在双曲空间中有效融合特征，为LLM生成的代码来源识别提供了有效的取证框架

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) trained on massive code corpora are now increasingly capable of generating code that is hard to distinguish from human-written code. This raises practical concerns, including security vulnerabilities and licensing ambiguity, and also motivates a forensic question: 'Who (or which LLM) wrote this piece of code?' We present GoCoMA, a multimodal framework that models an extrinsic hierarchy between (i) code stylometry, capturing higher-level structural and stylistic signatures, and (ii) image representations of binary pre-executable artifacts (BPEA), capturing lower-level, execution-oriented byte semantics shaped by compilation and toolchains. GoCoMA projects modality embeddings into a hyperbolic Poincaré ball, fuses them via a geodesic-cosine similarity-based cross-modal attention (GCSA) fusion mechanism, and back-projects the fused representation to Euclidean space for final LLM-source attribution. Experiments on two open-source benchmarks (CoDET-M4 and LLMAuthorBench) show that GoCoMA consistently outperforms unimodal and Euclidean multimodal baselines under identical evaluation protocols.

</details>


### [50] [Reciprocal Co-Training (RCT): Coupling Gradient-Based and Non-Differentiable Models via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16378)
*Yunshuo Tian,Akayou Kitessa,Tanuja Chitnis,Yijun Zhao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种通过强化学习将大语言模型与随机森林分类器耦合的互惠协同训练框架，使不兼容的模型家族能够通过双向适应相互利用优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型和传统机器学习方法在预测建模中具有互补优势，但由于它们根本不同的表示和训练范式（LLMs基于梯度优化的文本数据，而随机森林等模型使用不可微特征划分），阻碍了有效集成。

Method: 引入互惠协同训练框架，通过强化学习将LLM与随机森林分类器耦合，创建迭代反馈循环：将表格数据重新表述为标准化的文本表示供LLM使用，LLM的嵌入增强随机森林特征空间，而校准的随机森林概率估计提供反馈信号来指导LLM的强化学习更新。

Result: 在三个医学数据集上的实验表明，两个模型都获得了持续的性能提升，特别是LLM表现出特别强的效果。消融分析显示迭代细化、混合奖励设计和维度控制共同促成了这些增益。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了一种通用机制，允许不兼容的模型家族通过双向适应相互利用优势。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) and classical machine learning methods offer complementary strengths for predictive modeling, yet their fundamentally different representations and training paradigms hinder effective integration: LLMs rely on gradient-based optimization over textual data, whereas models such as Random Forests (RF) employ non-differentiable feature partitioning. This work introduces a reciprocal co-training framework that couples an LLM with an RF classifier via reinforcement learning, creating an iterative feedback loop in which each model improves using signals from the other. Tabular data are reformulated into standardized textual representations for the LLM, whose embeddings augment the RF feature space, while calibrated RF probability estimates provide feedback signals that guide reinforcement learning updates of the LLM. Experiments across three medical datasets demonstrate consistent performance gains for both models, with particularly strong effects for the LLM. Ablation analyses show that iterative refinement, hybrid reward design, and dimensionality control jointly contribute to these gains. The proposed framework provides a general mechanism that allows incompatible model families to leverage each other's strengths through bidirectional adaptation.

</details>


### [51] [Data Mixing for Large Language Models Pretraining: A Survey and Outlook](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16380)
*Zhuo Chen,Yuxuan Miao,Supryadi,Deyi Xiong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文对LLM预训练中的数据混合方法进行了系统性综述，将现有方法按静态/动态混合分类，分析了性能-成本权衡，并指出了跨方法挑战和未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据混合优化对LLM预训练效率和下游泛化能力具有决定性影响，但现有研究分散且缺乏系统性综述，需要建立统一的理论框架和分类体系来指导未来研究。

Method: 首先将数据混合优化形式化为概率单纯形上的双层优化问题，然后提出细粒度分类法：静态混合（规则基和学习基）和动态混合（自适应和外部引导），并分析各类方法的代表性技术和性能-成本权衡。

Result: 建立了数据混合方法的系统分类框架，识别了跨方法的核心挑战：跨数据域/优化目标/模型/验证集的有限可迁移性、评估协议和基准的非标准化、学习基方法中性能增益与成本控制的固有张力。

Conclusion: 数据混合是LLM预训练的关键优化方向，需要更精细的领域划分、逆向数据混合和管道感知设计等探索性方向，以推动该领域的概念和方法论发展。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) rely on pretraining on massive and heterogeneous corpora, where training data composition has a decisive impact on training efficiency and downstream generalization under realistic compute and data budget constraints. Unlike sample-level data selection, data mixing optimizes domain-level sampling weights to allocate limited budgets more effectively. In recent years, a growing body of work has proposed principled data mixing methods for LLM pretraining; however, the literature remains fragmented and lacks a dedicated, systematic survey. This paper provides a comprehensive review of data mixing for LLM pretraining. We first formalize data mixture optimization as a bilevel problem on the probability simplex and clarify the role of data mixing in the pretraining pipeline, and briefly explain how existing methods make this formulation tractable in practice. We then introduce a fine-grained taxonomy that organizes existing methods along two dimensions: static versus dynamic mixing. Static mixing is further categorized into rule-based and learning-based methods, while dynamic mixing is grouped into adaptive and externally guided families. For each class, we summarize representative approaches and analyze their strengths and limitations from a performance-cost trade-off perspective. Building on this analysis, we highlight challenges that cut across methods, including limited transferability across data domains, optimization objectives, models, and validation sets, as well as unstandardized evaluation protocols and benchmarks, and the inherent tension between performance gains and cost control in learning-based methods. Finally, we outline several exploratory directions, including finer-grained domain partitioning and inverse data mixing, as well as pipeline-aware designs, aiming to provide conceptual and methodological insights for future research.

</details>


### [52] [LiFT: Does Instruction Fine-Tuning Improve In-Context Learning for Longitudinal Modelling by Large Language Models?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16382)
*Iqra Ali,Talia Tseriotou,Mahmud Elahi Akhter,Yuxiang Zhou,Maria Liakata*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LiFT是一个纵向指令微调框架，通过统一的时间指令模式和渐进式课程学习，提升大语言模型在纵向NLP任务中的表现，特别是在处理历史上下文整合、演化交互追踪和罕见变化事件方面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大语言模型的上下文学习在纵向NLP任务中存在局限性，难以有效整合历史上下文、追踪演化交互和处理罕见变化事件，需要专门的方法来提升模型在时间序列文本分析中的能力。

Method: 提出LiFT框架：1）统一纵向建模任务到共享的指令模式；2）采用渐进式课程学习，逐步增加时间难度；3）结合少样本结构和时间条件，促进有效利用历史上下文；4）在不同时间粒度任务上进行训练。

Result: 在五个数据集上的评估显示，LiFT在不同参数规模的模型（OLMo 1B/7B、LLaMA-8B、Qwen-14B）上均优于基础模型的上下文学习，在分布外数据和少数变化事件上表现尤为突出，展现出良好的泛化能力。

Conclusion: LiFT框架通过专门的纵向指令微调和渐进式课程学习，显著提升了大语言模型在纵向NLP任务中的性能，特别是在处理复杂时间依赖和罕见事件方面，为时间序列文本分析提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Longitudinal NLP tasks require reasoning over temporally ordered text to detect persistence and change in human behavior and opinions. However, in-context learning with large language models struggles on tasks where models must integrate historical context, track evolving interactions, and handle rare change events. We introduce LiFT, a longitudinal instruction fine-tuning framework that unifies diverse longitudinal modeling tasks under a shared instruction schema. LiFT uses a curriculum that progressively increases temporal difficulty while incorporating few-shot structure and temporal conditioning to encourage effective use of past context. We evaluate LiFT across five datasets. Models trained on longitudinal tasks with different levels of temporal granularity are tested for generalisability on two separate datasets. Across models with different parameter sizes (OLMo (1B/7B), LLaMA-8B, and Qwen-14B), LiFT consistently outperforms base-model ICL, with strong gains on out-of-distribution data and minority change events.

</details>


### [53] [Measuring Representation Robustness in Large Language Models for Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16421)
*Vedant Jawandhia,Yash Sinha,Murari Mandal,Ankan Pal,Dhruv Kumar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: GeoRepEval框架评估LLMs在几何问题不同表示形式（欧几里得、坐标、向量）下的鲁棒性，发现表示选择可导致高达14%的准确率差异，向量表示是主要失败点，表明模型依赖表示特定启发式而非抽象几何推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有几何推理基准在固定格式上评估LLMs，隐含假设表示不变性，掩盖了仅因表示变化导致的失败。需要评估模型在不同问题表示（欧几里得、坐标、向量形式）下的鲁棒性，以理解其是否真正掌握抽象几何推理。

Method: 提出GeoRepEval评估框架，在158个高中几何问题（474个实例）上评估11个LLMs。框架测量问题级别的正确性、不变性和一致性，结合严格答案匹配、bootstrap置信区间、配对McNemar检验、表示翻转分析和表面复杂度回归控制。提出Invariance@3指标将准确率分解为鲁棒和脆弱成分。还测试了convert-then-solve提示干预策略。

Result: 表示选择可导致高达14个百分点的准确率差异。向量表示是持续失败点，Invariance@3低至0.044（即使控制长度和符号复杂度后）。convert-then-solve提示干预使高性能模型向量准确率提升高达52个百分点，表明失败源于表示敏感性而非能力不足；但低性能模型无改善，表明更深层限制。

Conclusion: 当前LLMs依赖表示特定启发式而非抽象几何推理。GeoRepEval框架揭示了表示不变性评估的重要性，为改进几何推理模型提供了诊断工具。所有数据集、提示和脚本已开源。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly evaluated on mathematical reasoning, yet their robustness to equivalent problem representations remains poorly understood. In geometry, identical problems can be expressed in Euclidean, coordinate, or vector forms, but existing benchmarks report accuracy on fixed formats, implicitly assuming representation invariance and masking failures caused by representational changes alone. We propose GeoRepEval, a representation-aware evaluation framework that measures correctness, invariance, and consistency at the problem level across parallel formulations, combining strict answer matching, bootstrap confidence intervals, paired McNemar tests, representation-flip analyses, and regression controls for surface complexity. We prove that our Invariance@3 metric decomposes accuracy into robust and fragile components and is bounded by the weakest representation. Evaluating eleven LLMs on 158 curated high-school geometry problems (474 instances), we find accuracy gaps of up to 14 percentage points induced solely by representation choice. Vector formulations emerge as a consistent failure point, with Invariance@3 as low as 0.044 even after controlling for length and symbolic complexity. A convert-then-solve prompting intervention improves vector accuracy by up to 52 percentage points for high-capacity models, suggesting that failures reflect representation sensitivity rather than inability; however, low-capacity models show no gains, indicating deeper limitations. These results suggest that current models rely on representation-specific heuristics rather than abstract geometric reasoning. All datasets, prompts, and scripts are released at https://github.com/vedjaw/GeoRepEval.

</details>


### [54] [Injecting Structured Biomedical Knowledge into Language Models: Continual Pretraining vs. GraphRAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16422)
*Jaafer Klila,Sondes Bannour Souihi,Rahma Boujelben,Nasredine Semmar,Lamia Hadrich Belguith*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究探索了两种利用UMLS结构化知识增强语言模型生物医学能力的方法：持续预训练将知识嵌入参数，以及GraphRAG在推理时查询知识图谱。构建了包含340万概念和3420万关系的大型生物医学知识图谱，并基于此训练了BERTUMLS和BioBERTUMLS模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将领域特定知识注入语言模型对于适应生物医学等专业领域至关重要。当前方法大多依赖非结构化文本语料，本研究探索利用UMLS Metathesaurus结构化知识的互补策略，以更有效地增强语言模型的生物医学能力。

Method: 1. 从UMLS构建大规模生物医学知识图谱（340万概念，3420万关系），存储在Neo4j中；2. 从图谱生成约1亿token的文本语料；3. 持续预训练两个模型：BERTUMLS（基于BERT）和BioBERTUMLS（基于BioBERT）；4. 开发GraphRAG管道，在推理时查询知识图谱；5. 在BLURB的六个数据集（五种任务类型）和两个QA数据集（PubMedQA、BioASQ）上评估。

Result: 1. BERTUMLS在BLURB任务上优于BERT，在知识密集型QA任务上提升最大；2. BioBERTUMLS对BioBERT的提升有限，表明基础模型已编码大量生物医学知识时收益递减；3. GraphRAG增强LLaMA 3-8B在PubMedQA上提升超过3个百分点，BioASQ上提升5个百分点，无需重新训练即可实现透明、多跳且易于更新的知识访问。

Conclusion: 结构化知识注入是增强语言模型生物医学能力的有效策略。持续预训练和GraphRAG是互补方法：前者将知识嵌入模型参数，后者在推理时提供灵活的知识访问。GraphRAG特别适合需要透明、可更新和多跳推理的应用场景。研究发布了处理后的UMLS Neo4j图谱以支持可重复性。

Abstract: The injection of domain-specific knowledge is crucial for adapting language models (LMs) to specialized fields such as biomedicine. While most current approaches rely on unstructured text corpora, this study explores two complementary strategies for leveraging structured knowledge from the UMLS Metathesaurus: (i) Continual pretraining that embeds knowledge into model parameters, and (ii) Graph Retrieval-Augmented Generation (GraphRAG) that consults a knowledge graph at inference time. We first construct a large-scale biomedical knowledge graph from UMLS (3.4 million concepts and 34.2 million relations), stored in Neo4j for efficient querying. We then derive a ~100-million-token textual corpus from this graph to continually pretrain two models: BERTUMLS (from BERT) and BioBERTUMLS (from BioBERT). We evaluate these models on six BLURB (Biomedical Language Understanding and Reasoning Benchmark) datasets spanning five task types and evaluate GraphRAG on the two QA (Question Answering) datasets (PubMedQA, BioASQ). On BLURB tasks, BERTUMLS improves over BERT, with the largest gains on knowledge-intensive QA. Effects on BioBERT are more nuanced, suggesting diminishing returns when the base model already encodes substantial biomedical text knowledge. Finally, augmenting LLaMA 3-8B with our GraphRAG pipeline yields over than 3 points accuracy on PubMedQA and 5 points on BioASQ without any retraining, delivering transparent, multi-hop, and easily updated knowledge access. We release the processed UMLS Neo4j graph to support reproducibility.

</details>


### [55] [HalluSAE: Detecting Hallucinations in Large Language Models via Sparse Auto-Encoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16430)
*Boshui Chen,Zhaoxin Fan,Ke Wang,Zhiying Leng,Faguo Wu,Hongwei Zheng,Yifan Sun,Wenjun Wu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: HalluSAE：基于相变理论的大语言模型幻觉检测框架，通过潜在能量景观建模将幻觉视为模型动态的临界转变


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有幻觉检测方法大多忽略了幻觉的动态特性和底层机制，限制了LLMs的实际应用效果。需要从动态系统角度理解幻觉的产生机制。

Method: 提出HalluSAE三阶段框架：1) 基于稀疏自编码器和几何势能度量的相区定位；2) 使用对比logit归因的幻觉相关稀疏特征归因；3) 通过解耦特征线性探针的因果幻觉检测

Result: 在Gemma-2-9B模型上的广泛实验表明，HalluSAE实现了最先进的幻觉检测性能

Conclusion: 将幻觉建模为潜在动态的临界转变，通过能量景观分析和稀疏特征归因，为理解LLMs幻觉机制提供了新视角和有效检测方法

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are powerful and widely adopted, but their practical impact is limited by the well-known hallucination phenomenon. While recent hallucination detection methods have made notable progress, we find most of them overlook the dynamic nature and underlying mechanisms of it. To address this gap, we propose HalluSAE, a phase transition-inspired framework that models hallucination as a critical shift in the model's latent dynamics. By modeling the generation process as a trajectory through a potential energy landscape, HalluSAE identifies critical transition zones and attributes factual errors to specific high-energy sparse features. Our approach consists of three stages: (1) Potential Energy Empowered Phase Zone Localization via sparse autoencoders and a geometric potential energy metric; (2) Hallucination-related Sparse Feature Attribution using contrastive logit attribution; and (3) Probing-based Causal Hallucination Detection through linear probes on disentangled features. Extensive experiments on Gemma-2-9B demonstrate that HalluSAE achieves state-of-the-art hallucination detection performance.

</details>


### [56] [SynopticBench: Evaluating Vision-Language Models on Generating Weather Forecast Discussions of the Future](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16451)
*Timothy B. Higgins,Antonios Mamalakis,Chirag Agarwal*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SynopticBench数据集和SPACE评估框架用于评估视觉语言模型在天气预报文本生成任务上的表现，揭示了现有评估指标在该领域的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然视觉语言模型在多种多模态任务上取得显著进展，但针对气象数据生成文本仍极具挑战性。大气是混沌系统，在时空尺度上快速变化，需要可靠量化现有VLMs在天气预报数据上的有效性。

Method: 提出了SynopticBench数据集（包含1,367,041个美国国家气象局区域预报讨论文本样本，配以500mb位势高度、2米温度和850mb风速预报图像）和SPACE评估框架（用于有效评估天气现象文本描述质量）。

Result: 在生成预报讨论的广泛实验中，展示了现有评估指标在该领域的敏感性，并为进一步探索天气和气候文本生成提供了基础。

Conclusion: SynopticBench数据集和SPACE评估框架为评估视觉语言模型在天气预报文本生成任务上的表现提供了高质量基准，揭示了该领域评估指标的局限性，推动了天气和气候文本生成研究的进一步发展。

Abstract: Recent advances in visual-language models (VLMs) have led to significant improvements in a plethora of complex multimodal tasks like image captioning, report generation, and visual perception. However, generating text from meteorological data is highly challenging because the atmosphere is a chaotic system that is rapidly changing at various spatial and temporal scales. Given the complexity of atmospheric phenomena, it is critical to verifiably quantify the effectiveness of existing VLMs on weather forecasting data. In this work, we present SynopticBench, a high-quality dataset consisting of 1,367,041 text samples of Area Forecast Discussions created by the National Weather Service over the continental United States paired to images of 500mb geopotential height, 2 meter temperature, and 850mb wind velocity in weather forecasts. We also present Synoptic Phenomena Alignment and Coverage Evaluation (SPACE), a novel evaluation framework that can be used to effectively estimate the quality of text descriptions of synoptic weather phenomena. Extensive experiments on generating forecast discussions using state-of-the-art VLMs show the sensitivity of existing evaluation metrics in this domain and enable further exploration into synoptic weather and climate text generation.

</details>


### [57] [EchoChain: A Full-Duplex Benchmark for State-Update Reasoning Under Interruptions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16456)
*Smit Nautambhai Modi,Gandharv Mahajan,Marc Wetter,Randall Welles*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: EchoChain是一个用于评估语音助手在实时全双工交互中处理中途打断时状态更新推理能力的基准测试，揭示了三种常见失败模式


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语音对话基准主要评估轮次式交互，忽略了用户在中途打断时助手需要修订任务状态的失败模式，需要专门基准来评估全双工状态更新推理能力

Method: 通过生成场景驱动的对话，在助手语音开始后的标准化时间点注入打断，创建受控的跨模型比较环境，并设置配对半双工对照组

Result: 识别出三种失败模式：上下文惯性、打断遗忘和目标偏移；半双工控制组失败率比打断组低40.2%；所有评估的实时语音模型通过率均未超过50%

Conclusion: EchoChain提供了一个可复现的基准，用于诊断全双工语音交互中的状态更新推理失败，揭示了当前模型在中途生成状态修订方面仍有很大改进空间

Abstract: Real-time voice assistants must revise task state when users interrupt mid-response, but existing spoken-dialog benchmarks largely evaluate turn-based interaction and miss this failure mode. We introduce EchoChain, a controlled benchmark for evaluating full-duplex state-update reasoning under mid-speech interruptions. EchoChain identifies three recurring failure patterns in post-interruption continuations: contextual inertia, interruption amnesia, and objective displacement. The benchmark generates scenario-driven conversations and injects interruptions at a standardized point relative to assistant speech onset, enabling controlled cross-model comparison. In a paired half-duplex control, total failures drop by 40.2% relative to interrupted runs, indicating that many errors are driven by state-update reasoning under interruption rather than task difficulty alone. Across evaluated real-time voice models, no system exceeds a 50% pass rate, showing substantial room for improvement in mid-generation state revision. EchoChain provides a reproducible benchmark for diagnosing state-update reasoning failures in full-duplex voice interaction.

</details>


### [58] [Revisiting a Pain in the Neck: A Semantic Reasoning Benchmark for Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16593)
*Yang Liu,Hongming Li,Melissa Xiaohui Qin,Qiankun Liu,Chao Huang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SemanticQA是一个评估语言模型在语义短语处理任务中表现的基准套件，整合了多词表达资源，涵盖词汇搭配、习语表达、名词复合词和动词结构等类别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需要评估语言模型在语义短语处理任务中的能力，特别是对非平凡语义短语的理解和推理能力，以揭示不同模型在语义理解方面的差异。

Method: 整合现有多词表达资源，重新组织为统一的测试平台，涵盖提取、分类、解释任务以及顺序任务组合，评估不同架构和规模的语言模型。

Result: 发现语言模型在需要语义推理的任务上表现存在显著差异，特别是在推理效能和语义理解方面，为提升语言模型对复杂语义短语的理解能力提供了见解。

Conclusion: SemanticQA基准套件能够有效评估语言模型在语义短语处理任务中的能力，揭示了模型在语义理解和推理方面的差异，为开发具有更强语义理解能力的语言模型提供了指导。

Abstract: We present SemanticQA, an evaluation suite designed to assess language models (LMs) in semantic phrase processing tasks. The benchmark consolidates existing multiword expression (MwE) resources and reorganizes them into a unified testbed. It covers both general lexical phenomena, such as lexical collocations, and three fine-grained categories: idiomatic expressions, noun compounds, and verbal constructions. Through SemanticQA, we assess LMs of diverse architectures and scales in extraction, classification, and interpretation tasks, as well as sequential task compositions. We reveal substantial performance variation, particularly on tasks requiring semantic reasoning, highlighting differences in reasoning efficacy and semantic understanding of LMs, providing insights for pushing LMs with stronger comprehension on non-trivial semantic phrases. The evaluation harness and data of SemanticQA are available at https://github.com/jacklanda/SemanticQA.

</details>


### [59] [Spotlights and Blindspots: Evaluation Machine-Generated Text Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16607)
*Kevin Stowe,Kailash Patil*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 评估了15个检测模型在多个数据集上的表现，发现没有单一系统在所有领域都优秀，模型性能受数据集和评估指标选择影响很大，在新型人类写作文本上表现较差


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着生成式语言模型的兴起，机器生成文本检测成为关键挑战。现有检测模型众多，但数据集、评估指标和评估策略的不一致使得模型效果难以比较

Method: 评估了来自6个不同系统的15个检测模型以及7个训练模型，在7个英文文本测试集和3个创意人类写作数据集上进行测试，分析模型性能、训练和评估数据的影响以及关键指标的影响

Result: 没有单一系统在所有领域都表现出色，几乎所有模型在某些任务上都有效；模型性能表现严重依赖于数据集和指标选择；不同数据集和指标下模型排名差异很大；在高风险领域的新型人类写作文本上整体表现较差

Conclusion: 方法论选择（通常被假设或忽视）对于清晰准确地反映模型性能至关重要；需要更标准化的评估框架来公平比较不同检测模型

Abstract: With the rise of generative language models, machine-generated text detection has become a critical challenge. A wide variety of models is available, but inconsistent datasets, evaluation metrics, and assessment strategies obscure comparisons of model effectiveness. To address this, we evaluate 15 different detection models from six distinct systems, as well as seven trained models, across seven English-language textual test sets and three creative human-written datasets. We provide an empirical analysis of model performance, the influence of training and evaluation data, and the impact of key metrics. We find that no single system excels in all areas and nearly all are effective for certain tasks, and the representation of model performance is critically linked to dataset and metric choices. We find high variance in model ranks based on datasets and metrics, and overall poor performance on novel human-written texts in high-risk domains. Across datasets and metrics, we find that methodological choices that are often assumed or overlooked are essential for clearly and accurately reflecting model performance.

</details>


### [60] [Aligning Backchannel and Dialogue Context Representations via Contrastive LLM Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16622)
*Livia Qian,Gabriel Skantze*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一个两阶段框架，通过微调大语言模型和联合嵌入空间学习，研究反馈信号（backchannels）的词汇-韵律形式与语用意义的关系，相比先前方法显著提升了上下文-反馈信号检索性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然先前计算研究主要关注预测反馈信号的时机，但词汇-韵律形式与意义之间的关系仍未得到充分探索。反馈信号（如"yeah"、"mhm"、"right"）作为简短的非打断性反馈信号，其词汇形式和韵律共同传达语用意义，需要更深入的研究。

Method: 提出两阶段框架：1）在对话转录文本上微调大语言模型以获得丰富的上下文表示；2）学习对话上下文和反馈信号实现的联合嵌入空间。通过三元相似性判断（韵律和跨词汇）以及上下文-反馈信号适宜性任务评估与人类感知的对齐程度。

Result: 学习到的投影相比先前方法显著改善了上下文-反馈信号检索性能。研究还发现反馈信号形式对扩展的对话上下文高度敏感，且学习到的嵌入比原始WavLM特征更接近人类判断。

Conclusion: 该研究成功开发了一个能够捕捉反馈信号词汇-韵律形式与语用意义关系的计算框架，证明了扩展对话上下文对反馈信号形式的重要性，并为更自然的对话系统提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Backchannels (e.g., `yeah', `mhm', and `right') are short, non-interruptive feedback signals whose lexical form and prosody jointly convey pragmatic meaning. While prior computational research has largely focused on predicting backchannel timing, the relationship between lexico-prosodic form and meaning remains underexplored. We propose a two-stage framework: first, fine-tuning large language models on dialogue transcripts to derive rich contextual representations; and second, learning a joint embedding space for dialogue contexts and backchannel realizations. We evaluate alignment with human perception via triadic similarity judgments (prosodic and cross-lexical) and a context-backchannel suitability task. Our results demonstrate that the learned projections substantially improve context-backchannel retrieval compared to previous methods. In addition, they reveal that backchannel form is highly sensitive to extended conversational context and that the learned embeddings align more closely with human judgments than raw WavLM features.

</details>


### [61] [Migrant Voices, Local News: Insights on Bridging Community Needs with Media Content](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16651)
*David Alonso del Barrio,Paula Dolores Rescala,Victor Bros,Daniel Gatica-Perez*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究通过焦点小组和自然语言处理分析，探讨法语移民对本地新闻的参与度及其需求在报道中的反映，发现报道存在主题缺口且语言可读性可能影响移民融入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究对非主流受众（特别是移民群体）在本地媒体中的新闻消费了解不足，需要填补这一研究空白，探索法语移民如何参与本地新闻以及他们的需求是否在报道中得到体现。

Method: 采用混合方法：1）8名社区成员参与焦点小组获取定性见解；2）基于焦点小组见解，对2000多篇超本地新闻文章应用自然语言处理方法，包括主题建模、信息检索、情感分析和可读性分析。

Result: 结果显示：1）文章频繁报道本地事件，但在参与者关注的重要主题上存在缺口；2）情感分析显示整体语气积极；3）可读性测量表明文章为中级-高级法语水平，可能影响移民的可及性和融入。

Conclusion: 该研究有助于弥合本地新闻平台内容与多样化读者需求之间的差距，可为本地媒体组织提供信息，帮助其扩展新闻报道范围以吸引更多样化的受众。

Abstract: Research shows news consumption differs across demographics, yet little is known about non-mainstream audiences, especially in relation to local media. Our study addresses this gap by examining how French-speaking migrants in a mid-size European city engage with local news, and whether their needs are reflected in coverage. Eight community members participated in focus groups, whose insights guided the selection of natural language processing methods (topic modeling, information retrieval, sentiment analysis, and readability) applied to over 2000 hyper-local news articles. Results showed that while articles frequently covered local events, gaps remained in topics important to participants. Sentiment analysis revealed a generally positive tone, and readability measures indicated an intermediate-advanced French level, raising questions about accessibility for integration. Our work contributes to bridging the gap between local news platforms' content and diverse readers' needs, and could inform local media organizations about opportunities to expand their current news story coverage to appeal to more diverse audiences.

</details>


### [62] [Defragmenting Language Models: An Interpretability-based Approach for Vocabulary Expansion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16656)
*Maitrey Mehta,Nishant Subramani,Zhichao Xu,Ashim Gupta,Vivek Srikumar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文研究LLM中非拉丁文字语言的token过度碎片化问题，提出基于可解释性的词汇扩展方法FragMend，通过分析子词解token化现象改进嵌入初始化，显著提升多语言处理效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当代LLM中，非拉丁文字语言存在严重的token过度碎片化问题，导致相同信息需要比英语多几倍的token，增加了访问成本和延迟。虽然词汇扩展是标准解决方案，但传统方法存在局限性，需要更有效的可解释性驱动方法。

Method: 提出基于可解释性的词汇扩展方法：1) 质疑传统基于频率的候选词汇选择方法，证明可解释性方法在性能-token效率权衡上更优；2) 分析"子词解token化"现象，即模型在跨层中逐步将碎片化子词token合并为更大子词；3) 基于此分析提出FragMend方法，改进嵌入初始化策略。

Result: 可解释性嵌入初始化方法相比基线方法在多个非拉丁文字语言上获得约20个百分点的显著提升。FragMend进一步提高了基于可解释性扩展的效率上限，通过强基线对比验证了其有效性，并对设计选择进行了广泛分析。

Conclusion: 基于可解释性的词汇扩展方法优于传统频率驱动方法，FragMend通过利用子词解token化现象进一步提升了非拉丁文字语言的处理效率，为LLM的多语言公平性提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: All languages are equal; when it comes to tokenization, some are more equal than others. Tokens are the hidden currency that dictate the cost and latency of access to contemporary LLMs. However, many languages written in non-Latin scripts observe a poor exchange rate: LLMs take several multiples of tokens to encode the same information in many languages as they do for English. Our analysis reveals that this issue, known as 'token over-fragmentation', persists in modern open-weight LLMs. The standard remedy is vocabulary expansion that adds target language items missing from the model's vocabulary. In this work, we comprehensively study and advance interpretability-based vocabulary expansion, a new research direction. We focus on two core decisions in the vocabulary expansion process: What items should we add? and How should we initialize their corresponding input and output embeddings? First, we question the conventional use of frequency-based methods to choose candidate vocabulary items to add (a decision long treated as settled), and show that interpretability-based methods offer a superior performance-token efficiency trade-off. Next, we strengthen the case for interpretability-based embedding initialization by showing large gains (~20 pts) over baseline initialization methods for several languages written in non-Latin scripts. We identify the phenomenon of "subword detokenization" where models progressively merge fragmented subword tokens into larger subwords across layers. Grounded in our analysis of this phenomenon, we propose FragMend to further push the efficiency ceiling of interpretability-based expansion. We validate the effectiveness of FragMend through comparison against strong baselines and we present extensive analysis of its design choices.

</details>


### [63] [CBRS: Cognitive Blood Request System with Bilingual Dataset and Dual-Layer Filtering for Multi-Platform Social Streams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16665)
*Anik Saha,Mst. Fahmida Sultana Naznin,Zia Ul Hassan Abdullah,Anisa Binte Asad,K. G. Subarno Bithi,A. B. M. Alim Al Islam*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CBRS是一个多平台认知血液请求系统，使用双层架构从社交媒体流中高效过滤和解析献血请求，在孟加拉语、英语和音译孟加拉语上达到99%过滤准确率，解析任务中LoRA微调的Llama-3.2-3B模型实现92%零样本准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交媒体上紧急献血请求常被海量信息淹没，传统应用系统依赖手动输入且在低资源环境下难以触达用户，导致关键响应延迟。需要开发高效系统来过滤和解析社交媒体上的献血请求。

Method: 提出认知血液请求系统(CBRS)，采用成本效益高的双层架构：1)过滤层识别献血请求，2)解析层提取关键信息。构建包含11K条解析献血请求消息的新数据集(孟加拉语、英语、音译孟加拉语)，包含对抗性负样本增强鲁棒性。使用LoRA微调Llama-3.2-3B模型进行解析任务。

Result: CBRS在过滤任务中达到99%准确率和精确率，超越基准方法。解析任务中，LoRA微调的Llama-3.2-3B模型实现92%零样本准确率，比基础模型提升41.54%，超过GPT-4o-mini、Gemini-2.0-Flash等LLM的少样本性能，同时减少35倍输入token使用量。

Conclusion: CBRS为时间敏感、对象聚焦任务中的可扩展、包容性信息提取奠定了坚实基础，能有效应对社交媒体上紧急献血请求的识别和解析挑战，特别适合低资源环境。代码、数据集和训练模型已开源。

Abstract: Urgent blood donation seeking posts and messages on social media often go unnoticed due to the overwhelming volume of daily communications. Traditional app-based systems, reliant on manual input, struggle to reach users in low-resource settings, delaying critical responses. To address this, we introduce the Cognitive Blood Request System (CBRS), a multi-platform framework that efficiently filters and parses blood donation requests from social media streams using a cost-efficient dual-layered architecture. To do so, we curate a novel dataset of 11K parsed blood donation request messages in Bengali, English, and transliterated Bengali, capturing the linguistic diversity of real social media communications. The inclusion of adversarial negatives further enhances the robustness of our model. CBRS achieves an impressive 99% accuracy and precision in filtering, surpassing benchmark methods. In the parsing task, our LoRA finetuned Llama-3.2-3B model achieves 92% zero-shot accuracy, surpassing the base model by 41.54% and exceeding the few-shot performance of GPT-4o-mini, Gemini-2.0-Flash, and other LLMs, while resulting in a 35X reduction in input token usage. This work lays a robust foundation for scalable, inclusive information extraction in time-sensitive, object-focused tasks. Our code, dataset, and trained models are publicly available at [https://github.com/aaniksahaa/CBRS](https://github.com/aaniksahaa/CBRS).

</details>


### [64] [No-Worse Context-Aware Decoding: Preventing Neutral Regression in Context-Conditioned Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16686)
*Yufei Tao,Ameeta Agrawal*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出NWCAD解码方法，防止LLMs在外部上下文非信息性时对已有正确答案产生中性回归，同时保持对有益上下文的利用能力


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在使用外部上下文（如检索证据）时存在可靠性问题：即使上下文是非信息性的，模型也可能覆盖已经正确的输出（中性回归）。需要解决这种"不造成伤害"的要求。

Method: 提出No-Worse Context-Aware Decoding (NWCAD)，这是一种基于双流设置的两阶段门控解码时间适配器：当上下文非信息性时回退到无上下文解码；否则使用上下文条件解码，并在不确定时采用CAD风格的回退机制。

Result: 在分离"不造成伤害"可靠性和上下文利用能力的基准测试中，NWCAD防止了基线正确项的中性回归，同时保持了在有益上下文上的强上下文驱动准确性。

Conclusion: NWCAD方法有效解决了LLMs使用外部上下文时的中性回归问题，实现了"不造成伤害"原则，同时不牺牲对真正有益上下文的利用能力。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can answer questions and summarize documents when conditioned on external contexts (e.g., retrieved evidence), yet context use remains unreliable: models may overwrite an already-correct output (neutral regression) even when the context is non-informative. We formalize neutral regression as a do-no-harm requirement and quantify it by measuring accuracy drops on baseline-correct items under answer-consistent contexts. We propose No-Worse Context-Aware Decoding (NWCAD), a decode-time adapter built on a two-stream setup with a two-stage gate: it backs off to no-context decoding when the context is non-informative, and otherwise uses context-conditioned decoding with a CAD-style fallback under uncertainty. We evaluate NWCAD on benchmarks that separate do-no-harm reliability from context utilization (accuracy gains on genuinely helpful contexts). NWCAD prevents neutral regression on baseline-correct items while preserving strong context-driven accuracy on helpful contexts.

</details>


### [65] [The impact of postediting on AI generative translation in Yemeni context: Translating literary prose by ChatGPT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16704)
*Nasim Al-wagieh,Mohammed Q. Shormani*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究探讨AI在文学翻译中的作用，发现ChatGPT-4虽能提升翻译速度和可及性，但在处理文化、风格和修辞方面仍有局限，需要人工译后编辑，特别是小说和戏剧翻译。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在评估人工智能（特别是ChatGPT-4）在文学翻译中的实际作用，探讨在多大程度上需要人工译后编辑，以及AI是否会取代人类译者。

Method: 采用混合方法，邀请30名专业译者评估和编辑AI生成的阿拉伯语和英语文学文本翻译，通过实际翻译任务和评估收集数据。

Result: 结果显示AI能提高翻译速度和可及性，但在处理文化内涵、风格特征和修辞手法方面存在明显局限，参与者普遍认为需要人工译后编辑，特别是在小说和戏剧翻译中。

Conclusion: AI应作为辅助工具而非人类译者的替代品，人类专业知识对确保翻译质量和文化适宜性仍然至关重要，未来应发展人机协作模式而非替代模式。

Abstract: This study examines the role of artificial intelligence in translation, focusing on ChatGPT, specifically ChatGPT-4, and the extent to which human postediting is required in literary translation. A mixed-method approach was adopted, involving 30 professional translators who evaluated and postedited AI-generated translations of selected Arabic and English literary texts. The results show that although AI improves translation speed and accessibility, it remains limited in handling cultural, stylistic, and figurative aspects of language. Participants generally confirmed the necessity of human postediting, particularly in novels and drama. The findings indicate that emerging human-machine collaboration model rather than replacement of human translators. The study concludes that AI should be used as a supportive tool, while human expertise remains essential for ensuring translation quality and cultural appropriateness.

</details>


### [66] [Detecting Alarming Student Verbal Responses using Text and Audio Classifier](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16717)
*Christopher Ormerod,Gitit Kehat*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一种结合文本分类器和音频分类器的混合框架，用于检测有困扰的学生，通过同时分析回答内容和韵律特征来改进传统自动语音评分系统的安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统自动语音评分系统存在关键安全漏洞，无法有效识别有困扰学生的回答。需要一种能够同时考虑回答内容和韵律特征的方法来检测潜在问题回答，以便及时干预。

Method: 提出新颖的混合框架：1) 文本分类器：基于回答内容进行检测；2) 音频分类器：基于韵律标记进行检测。结合两种分类器的优势，克服传统系统的局限性。

Result: 该方法在识别潜在问题回答方面实现了增强性能，能够更有效地检测有困扰的学生。系统可以加速人工审核过程，在需要及时干预的情况下可能挽救生命。

Conclusion: 提出的混合框架通过结合文本和音频分析，显著改进了自动语音评分系统的安全性，为及时识别和干预有困扰学生提供了有效工具。

Abstract: This paper addresses a critical safety gap in the use Automated Verbal Response Scoring (AVRS). We present a novel hybrid framework for troubled student detection that combines a text classifier, trained to detect responses based on their content, and an audio classifier, trained to detect responses using prosodic markers. This approach overcomes key limitations of traditional AVRS systems by considering both content and prosody of responses, achieving enhanced performance in identifying potentially concerning responses. This system can expedite the review process by humans, which can be life-saving particularly when timely intervention may be crucial.

</details>


### [67] [Evaluating Adaptive Personalization of Educational Readings with Simulated Learners](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16744)
*Ryan T. Woo,Anmol Rao,Aryan Keluskar,Yinong Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一个评估教育阅读材料自适应个性化框架，使用理论驱动的模拟学习者，通过构建学习目标和知识组件本体、标注教材内容、生成对齐的阅读-评估对，并利用模拟读者模型进行自适应阅读实验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需要系统评估教育阅读材料的自适应个性化效果，但传统实验成本高且难以控制变量。通过构建理论驱动的模拟学习者框架，可以在受控环境中高效评估不同学科领域的自适应阅读策略效果。

Method: 1) 从开放教材构建学习目标和知识组件本体；2) 在浏览器本体图谱中管理本体；3) 用本体实体标注教材片段；4) 生成对齐的阅读-评估对；5) 使用Construction-Integration记忆模型结合DIME读者因素、KREC错误概念修正和New Dale-Chall可读性信号的模拟读者；6) 基于学习者显性记忆状态的得分选项选择生成答案；7) 使用贝叶斯知识追踪(BKT)驱动自适应。

Result: 在三个学科本体和匹配的50名模拟学习者队列中：计算机科学自适应阅读显著改善学习效果；无机化学有较小正向但不显著的增益；普通生物学中自适应阅读效果中性或略微负面。

Conclusion: 自适应阅读个性化框架在不同学科领域效果不同，在计算机科学中效果显著，但在其他学科中效果有限或中性。这表明自适应策略需要针对特定学科领域进行优化，理论驱动的模拟学习者框架为高效评估教育个性化提供了可行方法。

Abstract: We present a framework for evaluating adaptive personalization of educational reading materials with theory-grounded simulated learners. The system builds a learning-objective and knowledge-component ontology from open textbooks, curates it in a browser-based Ontology Atlas, labels textbook chunks with ontology entities, and generates aligned reading-assessment pairs. Simulated readers learn from passages through a Construction-Integration-inspired memory model with DIME-style reader factors, KREC-style misconception revision, and an open New Dale-Chall readability signal. Answers are produced by score-based option selection over the learner's explicit memory state, while BKT drives adaptation. Across three sampled subject ontologies and matched cohorts of 50 simulated learners per condition, adaptive reading significantly improved outcomes in computer science, yielded smaller positive but inconclusive gains in inorganic chemistry, and was neutral to slightly negative in general biology.

</details>


### [68] [Expressing Social Emotions: Misalignment Between LLMs and Human Cultural Emotion Norms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16757)
*Sree Bhattacharyya,Manas Mehta,Leona Chen,Cristina Salvador,Agata Lapedriza,Shiran Dudy,James Z. Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LLMs在表达社会情感时存在系统性文化错位，无法准确反映人类跨文化的情感表达模式，特别是在表达参与性和疏离性情感方面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLMs越来越多地用于模拟具有文化细微差别的人类行为，需要了解它们是否能够准确捕捉人类社会情感表达的模式。当LLM响应与文化背景不一致时，其效用会受到损害，特别是当用户假设自己在与文化适应的对话者互动时，可能会采取在其文化背景下不合适的建议。

Method: 提出了一个心理学启发的跨文化社会情感表达评估框架。通过一项人类研究，比较了欧洲美国人和拉丁美洲参与者在表达参与性和疏离性情感方面的差异，并评估了六个前沿LLMs在反映文化差异化社会情感表达模式方面的能力。

Result: 发现模型与人类行为之间存在系统性错位：所有模型都更多地表达参与性情感而非疏离性情感，对于通常代表性较好的欧洲美国人角色，差异尤为明显。LLM响应高度集中和确定性，无法捕捉人类表达社会情感的多样性。消融分析显示这些模式对采样温度具有鲁棒性，对提示语言部分敏感，并依赖于响应引发格式。

Conclusion: 当前LLMs在表示文化和情感轴相互作用方面存在局限性，特别是在表达社会情感时，这对它们在跨文化情感背景下的部署具有直接影响。

Abstract: The expression of emotions that serve social purposes, such as asserting independence or fostering interdependence, is central to human interactions and varies systematically across cultures. As LLMs are increasingly used to simulate human behavior in culturally nuanced interactions, it is important to understand whether they faithfully capture human patterns of social emotion expression. When LLM responses are not culturally aligned, their utility is compromised -- particularly when users assume they are interacting with a culturally attuned interlocutor, and may act on advice that proves inappropriate in their cultural context. We present a psychologically informed evaluation framework of cross-cultural social emotion expression in LLMs. Using a human study comparing European American and Latin American participants' expression of engaging and disengaging emotions, we evaluate six frontier LLMs on their ability to reflect culturally differentiated patterns for expressing social emotions. We find systematic misalignment between model and human behavior: all models express engaging emotions more than disengaging ones, with particularly stark differences observed for the generally well-represented European American persona. We further highlight that LLM responses are highly concentrated and deterministic, failing to capture the diversity of human responses in expressing social emotions. Our ablation analyses reveal that these patterns are robust to sampling temperatures, partially sensitive to prompt language, and dependent on the response elicitation format. Together, our findings highlight limitations in how current LLMs represent the interaction of cultural and emotional axes, particularly when expressing social emotions, with direct implications for their deployment in cross-cultural affective contexts.

</details>


### [69] [When Misinformation Speaks and Converses: Rethinking Fact-Checking in Audio Platforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16767)
*Chaewan Chun,Delvin Ce Zhang,Dongwon Lee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 音频平台已成为错误信息传播的主要渠道，但现有事实核查方法主要针对文本内容，忽视了音频作为口语和对话媒体的独特属性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 音频平台（播客、电台、WhatsApp语音消息、直播等）已从娱乐工具演变为公共话语的核心平台，拥有数百万节目和数亿听众，成为错误信息传播的主要渠道。现有事实核查流程主要针对书面声明，忽视了音频作为口语和对话媒体的独特属性，导致对音频错误信息的检测和处理不足。

Method: 这是一篇立场论文，采用跨模态和跨平台的证据综合分析方法。通过审视现有数据集和方法，分析音频作为口语媒体（通过韵律、节奏、情感传递说服力）和对话媒体（跨越话轮、说话者和剧集展开）的双重属性，揭示传统事实核查方法在音频领域的局限性。

Result: 分析表明，音频错误信息不仅仅是带有转录的文本内容，它在结构上具有本质差异。音频的口语属性（韵律、节奏、情感）和对话属性（多话轮、多说话者、多剧集）引入了传统方法很少面临的验证困难，导致现有事实核查流程在音频领域效果不佳。

Conclusion: 推进事实核查工作需要围绕音频的口语和对话现实重新设计验证流程。必须认识到音频错误信息的独特结构特征，开发专门针对口语媒体属性的新方法，而不仅仅是依赖转录文本的传统文本分析方法。

Abstract: Audio platforms have evolved beyond entertainment. They have become central to public discourse, from podcasts and radio to WhatsApp voice notes and live streams. With millions of shows and hundreds of millions of listeners, audio platforms are now a major channel for misinformation. Yet existing fact-checking pipelines are mostly designed for written claims, overlooking the unique properties of spoken media. We argue that audio misinformation is not merely textual content with transcripts: it is structurally different because it is both spoken - carrying persuasive force through prosody, pacing, and emotion - and conversational - unfolding across turns, speakers, and episodes. These dual properties introduce verification difficulties that traditional methods rarely face. This position paper synthesizes evidence across modalities and platforms, examines datasets and methods, and highlights why existing pipelines fail on audio. We argue that advancing fact-checking requires rethinking verification pipelines around the spoken and conversational realities of audio.

</details>


### [70] [StageMem: Lifecycle-Managed Memory for Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16774)
*Jiarui Han*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: StageMem是一个生命周期管理的记忆框架，将记忆视为状态化过程而非被动存储库，通过三个阶段（瞬时、工作、持久记忆）和置信度/强度建模来解决LLM系统中记忆控制的实际问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM系统的记忆设计主要将记忆视为静态存储，这种框架无法充分捕捉实际部署中的记忆控制问题。实际困难通常不是忘记有用信息，而是保留了太多不确定项、以错误顺序忘记重要内容，以及用户对持久性内容缺乏信任。

Method: StageMem将记忆组织为三个阶段：瞬时记忆、工作记忆和持久记忆，并为每个记忆项建模明确的置信度和强度。这种设计将浅层接纳与长期承诺分离，允许信息先以低成本写入，随后根据证据和压力变化进行提升、保留、更新或淘汰。

Result: 在受控压力机制下，这种分解有助于保留后期重要内容，同时更好地控制记忆负担和深层污染。适应外部任务提供了边界证据，表明相同模式与纯合成控制之外更强的检索结构保持兼容。

Conclusion: StageMem为语言模型系统提供了一个原则性的记忆控制问题分解框架，将记忆视为状态化过程而非被动存储库，通过生命周期管理解决实际部署中的记忆控制挑战。

Abstract: Long-horizon language model systems increasingly rely on persistent memory, yet many current designs still treat memory primarily as a static store: write an item, place it into memory, and retrieve it later if needed. We argue that this framing does not adequately capture the practical memory-control problem in deployed LLM systems. In realistic settings, the difficulty is often not merely forgetting useful information, but retaining too many uncertain items, forgetting important content in the wrong order, and giving users little trust in what will persist over time. We propose StageMem, a lifecycle-managed memory framework that treats memory as a stateful process rather than a passive repository. StageMem organizes memory into three stages -- transient, working, and durable memory -- and models each item with explicit confidence and strength. This separates shallow admission from long-term commitment: information may first be written at low cost and only later be promoted, retained, updated, or evicted as evidence and pressure evolve. Under controlled pressure regimes, this decomposition helps preserve late-important content while keeping memory burden and deeper-tier pollution more controlled. Adapted external tasks provide boundary evidence that the same schema remains compatible with stronger retrieval structure outside pure synthetic control. We present StageMem as a principled decomposition of the memory-control problem for language model systems.

</details>


### [71] [Crowded in B-Space: Calibrating Shared Directions for LoRA Merging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16826)
*Yixuan Tang,Yi Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出Pico方法，通过区分LoRA矩阵B和A，校准输出侧矩阵B的过度共享方向，改善LoRA适配器合并性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LoRA合并方法将ΔW=BA视为单一对象，不区分两个LoRA矩阵，导致合并后性能下降。研究发现干扰主要来自输出侧矩阵B，其过度使用共享方向导致任务特定信息丢失

Method: 提出Pico（预合并干扰校准）方法：1）识别B矩阵中的过度共享方向；2）在合并前对这些方向进行降尺度处理；3）重新缩放合并后的更新。该方法无需数据，可直接集成到现有合并方法（Task Arithmetic、TIES、TSV-M）中

Result: 在数学、编码、金融和医疗等8个基准测试中，Pico将平均准确率提高了3.4-8.3个百分点，达到最佳整体平均性能。合并后的适配器甚至优于使用所有任务数据训练的LoRA

Conclusion: LoRA合并在处理两个LoRA矩阵时需分别对待，Pico通过校准B矩阵的共享方向有效减少干扰，显著提升合并性能，证明区分LoRA矩阵的重要性

Abstract: Merging separately trained LoRA adapters is a practical alternative to joint multi-task training, but it often hurts performance. Existing methods usually treat the LoRA update $ΔW = BA$ as a single object and do not distinguish the two LoRA matrices. We show that the main source of LoRA merge interference comes from the output-side matrix $B$. Across tasks, $B$ repeatedly uses a small set of shared directions, while $A$ remains much more task-specific. As a result, the merged adapter overemphasizes these shared directions, and task-specific information is lost. We propose Pico (Pre-merge interference calibration in output-space), a data-free method that calibrates $B$ before merge by downscaling over-shared directions and then rescaling the merged update. Pico plugs directly into existing merging methods such as Task Arithmetic, TIES, and TSV-M. Across eight different benchmarks from math, coding, finance, and medical domains, Pico improves average accuracy by 3.4-8.3 points over the corresponding base method and achieves the best overall average performance. Pico also enables merged adapters to outperform the LoRA trained with all task data. These results show that LoRA merging works better when the two LoRA matrices are treated separately.

</details>


### [72] [DART: Mitigating Harm Drift in Difference-Aware LLMs via Distill-Audit-Repair Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16845)
*Ziwen Pan,Zihan Liang,Jad Kabbara,Ali Emami*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出DART方法解决LLM在安全微调后对人口统计学差异的"身份盲视"问题，通过差异意识分类和修复训练，在提升准确性的同时避免有害内容漂移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对安全微调后的大型语言模型往往避免承认人口统计学差异，即使这些差异是事实正确或情境合理的。这种"身份盲视"导致错误回答、不必要的拒绝或通用的"平等对待"默认响应。需要解决准确性与安全性之间的冲突。

Method: 提出DART（Distill--Audit--Repair Training）方法：1）从教师模型蒸馏标签条件推理；2）相对于基线审计输出的有害漂移情况；3）通过严重性加权微调修复问题案例。引入差异意识分类任务，判断问题是否需要识别群体差异。

Result: 在8个基准测试中，DART将Llama-3-8B-Instruct的准确率从39.0%提升至68.8%，在平等对待提示上提升最大（11.3%→72.6%），同时减少72.6%的有害漂移案例。在280个开放领域真实查询中，差异适当响应从39.8%提升至77.5%，拒绝率从34.3%降至3.0%。

Conclusion: 研究表明，通过明确的检测和修复机制，准确性和安全性可以不必冲突。DART方法有效解决了LLM在处理人口统计学差异时的身份盲视问题，同时避免了有害内容漂移。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) tuned for safety often avoid acknowledging demographic differences, even when such acknowledgment is factually correct (e.g., ancestry-based disease incidence) or contextually justified (e.g., religious hiring preferences). This identity-blindness yields incorrect responses, unnecessary refusals, or generic "equal-treatment" defaults. We study this via difference-awareness classification: given a question involving demographic groups, the task is not to answer directly, but to classify whether a correct answer requires recognizing group differences (yes) or whether groups should be treated identically (no). Crucially, fine-tuning for accuracy triggers harm drift: model-generated explanations become increasingly harmful as decision accuracy improves, whether by elaborating harmful content, introducing problematic assumptions, or failing to flag harms the baseline identified. To mitigate this, we introduce DART (Distill--Audit--Repair Training), which distills label-conditioned reasoning from a teacher, audits outputs for harm drift cases relative to baseline, and repairs problematic cases via severity-weighted fine-tuning. On eight benchmarks, DART improves Llama-3-8B-Instruct accuracy from 39.0% to 68.8%, with largest gains on equal-treatment prompts (11.3% -> 72.6%), while reducing harm drift cases by 72.6%. It also transfers to 280 open-ended real-world queries across medical, legal, policy, and educational domains, improving difference-appropriate responses from 39.8% to 77.5% while reducing refusals from 34.3% to 3.0%. Our results demonstrate that accuracy and safety need not conflict when explicit detection and repair mechanisms are in place.

</details>


### [73] [A Community-Based Approach for Stance Distribution and Argument Organization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16852)
*Rudra Ranajee Saha,Laks V. S. Lakshmanan,Raymond T. Ng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一种基于图的无监督方法，用于组织社区化论证，通过多类型关系构建交互图并检测论证社区，帮助用户理解和导航复杂的论证景观。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线辩论平台和社交媒体的激增产生了大量关于争议话题的多视角论证内容。虽然这些丰富视角为培养批判性思维和打破信息茧房提供了机会，但论证的数量和复杂性使得读者难以有效综合和理解多样观点。

Method: 采用无监督的基于图的方法进行社区化论证组织。系统分析主题聚焦的文章集合，通过捕捉论证间的多种关系类型（主题相似性、语义连贯性、共享关键词、共同实体）构建丰富的交互图。然后使用社区检测识别论证社区，揭示同质和异质的观点分布。检测到的社区通过战略性的图操作进行简化，向用户呈现易于消化但全面的关键论证模式摘要。

Result: 实验结果表明，该系统能够识别有意义的论证社区，并以可解释的方式呈现，促进用户对复杂社会政治辩论的理解。该方法无需训练数据，能够有效处理数百篇文章，同时保持论证间的细微关系。

Conclusion: 提出的无监督图方法为组织复杂论证景观提供了一种有效解决方案，通过社区检测和简化操作帮助用户导航和理解多视角论证内容，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: The proliferation of online debate platforms and social media has led to an unprecedented volume of argumentative content on controversial topics from multiple perspectives. While this wealth of perspectives offers opportunities for developing critical thinking and breaking filter bubbles (Pariser 2011), the sheer volume and complexity of arguments make it challenging for readers to synthesize and comprehend diverse viewpoints effectively. We present an unsupervised graph-based approach for community-based argument organization that helps users navigate and understand complex argumentative landscapes. Our system analyzes collections of topic-focused articles and constructs a rich interaction graph by capturing multiple relationship types between arguments: topic similarity, semantic coherence, shared keywords, and common entities. We then employ community detection to identify argument communities that reveal homogeneous and heterogeneous viewpoint distributions. The detected communities are simplified through strategic graph operations to present users with digestible, yet comprehensive summaries of key argumentative patterns. Our approach requires no training data and can effectively process hundreds of articles while preserving nuanced relationships between arguments. Experimental results demonstrate our system's ability to identify meaningful argument communities and present them in an interpretable manner, facilitating users' understanding of complex socio-political debates.

</details>


### [74] [Incentivizing Parametric Knowledge via Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards for Cross-Cultural Entity Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16881)
*Jiang Zhou,Xiaohu Zhao,Xinwei Wu,Tianyu Dong,Hao Wang,Yangyang Liu,Heng Liu,Linlong Xu,Longyue Wang,Weihua Luo,Deyi Xiong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: EA-RLVR框架通过实体锚定强化学习和可验证奖励，优化LLM的跨文化实体翻译能力，无需外部知识库，显著提升翻译准确性和泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在跨文化实体翻译中通常产生字面或音译结果，而非上下文中的文化适宜翻译。虽然相关知识可能已在预训练中编码，但需要有效激励模型利用这些参数化知识。

Method: 提出EA-RLVR（实体锚定强化学习与可验证奖励）训练框架，通过实体级别的可验证奖励信号进行监督，结合轻量级结构化门控稳定优化，引导模型学习稳健的推理过程而非简单模仿参考翻译。

Result: 在XC-Translate数据集上评估显示，仅用7k样本训练即可将Qwen3-14B的实体翻译准确率从23.66%提升至31.87%（50k测试集包含完全未见实体）。学习到的能力还能迁移到通用翻译，在WMT24++上获得+1.35 XCOMET提升，扩展优化后达+1.59。

Conclusion: EA-RLVR通过实体锚定奖励和结构化门控实现了高效的采样和稳定优化，显著提升了LLM的跨文化实体翻译能力，且该能力具有良好的泛化性和迁移性。

Abstract: Cross-cultural entity translation remains challenging for large language models (LLMs) as literal or phonetic renderings are usually yielded instead of culturally appropriate translations in context. However, relevant knowledge may already be encoded in model parameters during large-scale pre-training. To incentivize the effective use of parametric knowledge, we propose EA-RLVR (Entity-Anchored Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards), a training framework that optimizes cross-cultural entity translation without relying on external knowledge bases. EA-RLVR anchors supervision on a verifiable, entity-level reward signal and incorporates lightweight structural gates to stabilize optimization. This design steers the model toward learning a robust reasoning process rather than merely imitating reference translations. We evaluate EA-RLVR on XC-Translate and observe consistent improvements in both entity translation accuracy and out-of-domain generalization. Specifically, training on merely 7k samples boosts Qwen3-14B's entity translation accuracy from 23.66\% to 31.87\% on a 50k test set comprising entirely unseen entities. The learned entity translation ability also transfers to general translation, yielding +1.35 XCOMET on WMT24++, which scales to +1.59 with extended optimization. Extensive analyses of $pass@k$ dynamics and reward formulations attribute these gains to superior sampling efficiency and a stable optimization landscape.

</details>


### [75] [Prune, Interpret, Evaluate: A Cross-Layer Transcoder-Native Framework for Efficient Circuit Discovery via Feature Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16889)
*Qinhao Chen,Linyang He,Nima Mesgarani*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: PIE框架：首个跨层transcoder特征剪枝-解释-评估端到端系统，通过FAP和FAP-Synergy方法实现40倍特征压缩，显著降低解释成本


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有特征解释流程通常处理均匀采样的单元，但只有少量跨层transcoder特征对目标行为重要，其余特征导致昂贵的解释和评估成本。需要系统化方法在剪枝条件下测量行为保真度和下游可解释性。

Method: 提出PIE框架，整合剪枝、自动解释和解释评估。核心是特征归因修补(FAP)，一种基于补丁的归因方法，通过聚合梯度加权写入贡献来评分CLT特征；以及FAP-Synergy，一种协同感知的重新排序过程。使用KL散度行为保留评估剪枝，使用FADE风格指标评估解释质量。

Result: 在IOI和Doc-String任务上，在不同预算K∈{50,100,200,400,800}和不同剪枝方法下，FAP系列始终达到最佳或接近最佳保真度。在IOI任务中，对Llama-3.2-1B和Gemma-2-2B的CLT，剪枝到K=100特征匹配了从活跃特征集中随机选择约4k特征所需的KL保真度（约40倍压缩），减少约40倍解释/评估调用，同时显著减少低质量特征。

Conclusion: PIE是首个CLT原生的端到端框架，通过FAP和FAP-Synergy方法实现了高效的特征剪枝，在严格预算下提供清晰优势，显著降低了特征解释的计算成本，同时保持行为保真度。

Abstract: Existing feature-interpretation pipelines typically operate on uniformly sampled units, but only a small fraction of cross-layer transcoder (CLT) features matter for a target behavior, with the rest resulting in expensive feature explaining and evaluating costs. We introduce the first CLT-native end-to-end framework, PIE, connecting Pruning, automatic Interpretation, and interpretation Evaluation, enabling systematic measurement of behavioral fidelity and downstream interpretability under pruning. To achieve this, we propose Feature Attribution Patching (FAP), a patch-grounded attribution method that scores CLT features by aggregating gradient-weighted write contributions, and FAP-Synergy, a synergy-aware reranking procedure. We evaluate pruning using KL-divergence behavior retention and assess interpretation quality with FADE-style metrics.
