Across IOI and Doc-String, across budgets $K \in \{50, 100, 200, 400, 800\}$, and across FAP, FAP-Synergy, Activation-Magnitude, and ACDC-style pruning, the FAP family consistently achieves the best or near-best fidelity, with FAP-Synergy providing its clearest gains in strict-budget regimes. On IOI with CLTs for Llama-3.2-1B and Gemma-2-2B, pruning to $K=100$ features matches the KL fidelity that random selection from the active feature set requires $\approx 4$k features to achieve ($\approx 40\times$ compression), enabling $\approx 40\times$ fewer interpretation/evaluation calls while substantially reducing low-quality features.

</details>


### [76] [When Choices Become Risks: Safety Failures of Large Language Models under Multiple-Choice Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16916)
*Yuheng Chen,Zhiyu Wu,Bowen Cheng,Tetsuro Takahashi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究发现大语言模型在结构化决策任务（如选择题）中存在系统性安全漏洞：将有害请求重新表述为强制选择选择题可以绕过模型的拒绝行为，即使模型在开放式生成中会拒绝相同内容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大语言模型的安全对齐主要评估开放式生成场景，但许多实际应用将LLMs置于结构化决策任务中，其中弃权选项不可用。研究者发现现有安全评估可能严重低估了结构化任务设置中的风险。

Method: 通过将有害请求重新表述为强制选择选择题（所有选项都不安全），测试14个专有和开源模型。研究人类编写的选择题和由高能力模型生成的选择题，分析结构约束强度与违规率之间的关系。

Result: 强制选择约束显著增加了违反安全政策的响应。人类编写的选择题违规率呈现倒U型趋势，在中等任务规范下达到峰值；而由高能力模型生成的选择题在所有约束下都接近饱和违规率，并表现出强大的跨模型可转移性。

Conclusion: 当前安全评估严重低估了结构化任务设置中的风险，约束决策是安全对齐失败的关键且未被充分探索的表面。研究揭示了LLMs在结构化决策环境中的系统性安全漏洞。

Abstract: Safety alignment in large language models (LLMs) is primarily evaluated under open-ended generation, where models can mitigate risk by refusing to respond. In contrast, many real-world applications place LLMs in structured decision-making tasks, such as multiple-choice questions (MCQs), where abstention is discouraged or unavailable. We identify a systematic failure mode in this setting: reformulating harmful requests as forced-choice MCQs, where all options are unsafe, can systematically bypass refusal behavior, even in models that consistently reject equivalent open-ended prompts. Across 14 proprietary and open-source models, we show that forced-choice constraints sharply increase policy-violating responses. Notably, for human-authored MCQs, violation rates follow an inverted U-shaped trend with respect to structural constraint strength, peaking under intermediate task specifications, whereas MCQs generated by high-capability models yield near-saturation violation rates across constraints and exhibit strong cross-model transferability. Our findings reveal that current safety evaluations substantially underestimate risks in structured task settings and highlight constrained decision-making as a critical and underexplored surface for alignment failures.

</details>


### [77] [x1: Learning to Think Adaptively Across Languages and Cultures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16917)
*Yangfan Ye,Xiaocheng Feng,Xiachong Feng,Yichong Huang,Zekun Yuan,Lei Huang,Weitao Ma,Qichen Hong,Yunfei Lu,Dandan Tu,Bing Qin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: x1模型家族通过自适应选择推理语言提升多语言推理能力，挑战了简单扩展定律，证明语言选择是推理的功能性组件


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型通常在单一主导语言中进行推理，忽视了不同语言编码的独特抽象和归纳先验的多样性，限制了模型的全球能力和文化适应性

Method: 构建x1推理模型家族，通过对比同一输入的不同语言推理轨迹进行训练，自适应选择优势语言进行实例级推理，同时不扩展模型的知识边界以隔离语言选择效应

Result: 自适应多语言推理在多语言数学推理和文化相关任务中均表现出优势；扩展定律在数学推理等程序性领域能减少跨语言差异，但在文化相关任务中无法消除文化关联语言的优势，后者能实现更高效准确的文化知识回忆

Conclusion: 语言选择是推理的功能性组件，这一发现对构建更通用和全球胜任的推理模型具有重要意义，挑战了简单的扩展定律观点

Abstract: Languages encode distinct abstractions and inductive priors, yet most large language models (LLMs) overlook this diversity by reasoning in a single dominant language. In this work, we introduce x1, a family of reasoning models that can adaptively reason in an advantageous language on a per-instance basis. To isolate the effect of reasoning-language choice, x1 is constructed without expanding the model's knowledge boundaries and is trained by contrasting linguistically distinct reasoning trajectories for the same input. Our extensive experiments demonstrate the benefits of adaptive multilingual reasoning across multilingual mathematical reasoning and culturally grounded tasks. Moreover, our results challenge a simplistic view of scaling laws: while scaling reduces cross-lingual disparities in procedural domains such as math reasoning, it does not eliminate the advantages of culture-associated languages in culturally grounded tasks, as we empirically show that such reasoning enables more efficient and accurate cultural knowledge recall. Overall, our findings establish language choice as a functional component of reasoning, with implications for building more generalist and globally competent reasoning models.

</details>


### [78] [Freshness-Aware Prioritized Experience Replay for LLM/VLM Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16918)
*Weiyu Ma,Yongcheng Zeng,Yan Song,Xinyu Cui,Jian Zhao,Xuhui Liu,Mohamed Elhoseiny*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了Freshness-Aware PER方法，通过引入基于有效样本量的指数年龄衰减来解决大语言模型强化学习中优先级经验回放存在的优先级过时问题，显著提升了多步智能体任务的样本效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大语言模型和视觉语言模型的强化学习主要采用PPO、GRPO、REINFORCE++等在线策略算法，这些方法在单次梯度更新后丢弃所有收集的轨迹，导致样本效率低下。特别是在多步环境交互成本高昂的智能体任务中，这种浪费尤为严重。虽然经典强化学习中经验回放通过重用历史轨迹和优先化信息丰富的样本来提高样本效率，但直接将优先级经验回放应用于大语言模型会失败，因为数十亿参数模型的策略快速演化使得存储的优先级迅速过时。

Method: 提出了Freshness-Aware PER方法，通过将任何基于PER的优先级与基于有效样本量分析的乘法指数年龄衰减相结合，解决了优先级过时问题。该方法在计算轨迹优先级时考虑了轨迹的"新鲜度"，确保旧的高优先级轨迹不会在变得无信息后长期主导采样。

Result: 在8个多步智能体、推理和数学竞赛任务上评估了0.5B、3B和7B模型。Freshness-Aware PER显著优于在线策略基线：在NQ Search上提升46%，在Sokoban上提升367%，在VLM FrozenLake上提升133%。相比之下，没有年龄衰减的标准PER会持续降低性能。

Conclusion: Freshness-Aware PER是首个成功将优先级经验回放应用于LLM/VLM强化学习的工作，通过解决优先级过时问题，显著提高了大语言模型在多步智能体任务中的样本效率和性能。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning (RL) has achieved impressive success in post-training Large Language Models (LLMs) and Vision-Language Models (VLMs), with on-policy algorithms such as PPO, GRPO, and REINFORCE++ serving as the dominant paradigm. However, these methods discard all collected trajectories after a single gradient update, resulting in poor sample efficiency, particularly wasteful for agentic tasks where multi-turn environment interactions are expensive. While Experience Replay drives sample efficiency in classic RL by allowing agents to reuse past trajectories and prioritize informative ones, directly applying Prioritized Experience Replay (PER) to LLMs fails. The rapid policy evolution of billion-parameter models renders stored priorities stale, causing old high-priority trajectories to dominate sampling long after they have become uninformative. We propose Freshness-Aware PER, which addresses this priority staleness problem by augmenting any PER-based priority with a multiplicative exponential age decay grounded in effective sample size analysis. To the best of our knowledge, Freshness-Aware PER is the first work to successfully apply PER to LLM/VLM reinforcement learning. We evaluate on eight multi-step agentic, reasoning, and math competition tasks with 0.5B, 3B, and 7B models. Freshness-Aware PER significantly outperforms on-policy baselines, achieving +46% on NQ Search, +367% on Sokoban, and +133% on VLM FrozenLake, while standard PER without age decay consistently degrades performance. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/Vision-CAIR/Freshness-Aware-PER.

</details>


### [79] [MeasHalu: Mitigation of Scientific Measurement Hallucinations for Large Language Models with Enhanced Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16929)
*Ruijun Huang,Zhiqiao Kang,Yuxuan Zhu,Junxiong Li,Jiahao Zhao,Minghuan Tan,Feng Jiang,Min Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MeasHalu框架通过增强推理和针对性优化来减少科学测量提取中的幻觉问题，显著提高自动化科学文献分析的可靠性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从科学文献中准确提取测量数据是AI4Science的关键挑战，但大型语言模型存在严重幻觉问题，这显著削弱了自动化科学文档理解系统的可靠性

Method: 提出MeasHalu框架：1）建立细粒度的测量特定幻觉分类法；2）采用两阶段推理感知微调策略，使用增强的科学数据和过程监督；3）引入渐进式奖励课程，针对特定幻觉类型进行惩罚

Result: 实验结果表明，MeasHalu显著降低了幻觉率，并在MeasEval基准上提高了整体准确性

Conclusion: 这项工作为自动化科学知识提取中的关键瓶颈提供了针对性解决方案，促进了更可信和可扩展的机器辅助科学文献分析

Abstract: The accurate extraction of scientific measurements from literature is a critical yet challenging task in AI4Science, enabling large-scale analysis and integration of quantitative research findings. However, Large Language Models (LLMs) frequently exhibit severe hallucinations, which significantly undermine the reliability of automated scientific document understanding systems. To address this problem, we propose MeasHalu, a novel framework for mitigating scientific measurement hallucinations through enhanced reasoning and targeted optimization. We first present a fine-grained taxonomy of measurement-specific hallucinations, categorizing errors across quantities, units, modifiers, and relations. Our approach incorporates a two-stage reasoning-aware fine-tuning strategy using augmented scientific data and process-based supervision. Furthermore, we introduce a progressive reward curriculum designed to penalize specific hallucination types, significantly improving extraction faithfulness. Experimental results demonstrate that MeasHalu substantially reduces hallucination rates and improves overall accuracy on the MeasEval benchmark. This work provides a targeted solution to a key bottleneck in automated scientific knowledge extraction, facilitating more trustworthy and scalable machine-assisted scientific literature analysis.

</details>


### [80] [No One Fits All: From Fixed Prompting to Learned Routing in Multilingual LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16937)
*Wei-Chi Wu,Sheng-Lun Wei,Hen-Hsen Huang,Hsin-Hsi Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文评估了多语言LLM中的翻译提示策略，发现没有单一最优策略，低资源语言从翻译中获益最大，高资源语言获益有限，并提出了基于学习的策略选择分类器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 翻译提示在多语言LLM中被广泛使用，但其效果在不同语言和任务中存在差异，需要系统评估不同策略的有效性并开发更智能的提示策略选择方法。

Method: 在10种不同资源水平的语言和4个基准测试上评估提示策略，分析翻译提示的效果，并引入轻量级分类器来预测每个实例的最优提示策略（原生或翻译）。

Result: 翻译对低资源语言有显著帮助（即使翻译质量不完美），高资源语言获益有限，基于提示的自路由策略效果不佳。学习型分类器在四个基准测试上相比固定策略取得统计显著改进，并能泛化到未见过的任务格式。

Conclusion: 语言资源水平而非翻译质量单独决定了翻译提示的益处，学习型策略选择比固定策略更有效，为多语言LLM提示策略提供了数据驱动的决策框架。

Abstract: Translation-based prompting is widely used in multilingual LLMs, yet its effectiveness varies across languages and tasks. We evaluate prompting strategies across ten languages of different resource levels and four benchmarks. Our analysis shows that no single strategy is universally optimal. Translation strongly benefits low-resource languages even when translation quality is imperfect, high-resource languages gain little, and prompt-based self-routing underperforms explicit translation. Motivated by these findings, we formulate prompting strategy selection as a learned decision problem and introduce lightweight classifiers that predict whether native or translation-based prompting is optimal for each instance. The classifiers achieve statistically significant improvements over fixed strategies across four benchmarks and generalize to unseen task formats not observed during training. Further analysis reveals that language resource level, rather than translation quality alone, determines when translation is beneficial.

</details>


### [81] [MNAFT: modality neuron-aware fine-tuning of multimodal large language models for image translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16943)
*Bo Li,Ningyuan Deng,Tianyu Dong,Shaobo Wang,Shaolin Zhu,Lijie Wen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MNAFT通过识别MLLMs中的语言无关和语言特定神经元，进行选择性微调，显著提升图像翻译性能，避免预训练知识冗余


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型在图像翻译中难以有效捕捉图像中的细粒度文本信息，导致视觉文本输入与文本输入/输出之间存在模态鸿沟。现有方法主要依赖指令微调，存在预训练知识参数冗余问题，影响泛化性能。

Method: 提出模态神经元感知微调(MNAFT)：1)通过指令驱动激活分析识别视觉和语言模块中的语言无关和语言特定神经元；2)评估这些神经元在不同翻译任务中的重要性；3)选择性微调目标任务相关层中的语言特定和语言无关神经元参数，同时保留其他神经元和层的知识。

Result: 在多个基准测试上的广泛实验表明，MNAFT显著优于最先进的图像翻译方法，包括级联模型、标准全微调和参数高效调优技术。同时提供了神经元激活和聚类模式的可视化分析。

Conclusion: MNAFT通过利用MLLMs中神经元的专门角色进行选择性微调，有效解决了图像翻译中的模态鸿沟问题，在保持预训练知识的同时提升了翻译准确性，为跨模态理解和语言特定翻译提供了新的见解。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have shown impressive capabilities, yet they often struggle to effectively capture the fine-grained textual information within images crucial for accurate image translation. This often leads to a modality gap between visual text inputs and textual inputs/outputs for image translation. Existing methods, primarily relying on instruction fine-tuning, risk parameter redundancy of pre-trained knowledge, hindering generalization performance. To address this, we introduce modality neuron-aware fine-tuning (MNAFT), a novel approach that takes advantage of the specialized roles of individual neurons within MLLMs for enhanced image translation. MNAFT identifies language-agnostic and language-specific neurons in both vision and language modules through an instruction-driven activation analysis, evaluating their importance in various translation tasks. We then perform selective fine-tuning, updating only the parameters of language-specific and language-agnostic neurons within the selected layers relevant to the target task, while preserving the knowledge encoded in other neurons and layers. Our extensive experiments on multiple benchmarks demonstrate that MNAFT significantly outperforms state-of-the-art image translation methods, including cascaded models, standard full fine-tuning, and parameter-efficient tuning techniques. Furthermore, we provide comprehensive analysis, including visualizations of neuron activations and clustering patterns, to offer insights into the roles of different neuron groups in mediating cross-modal understanding and facilitating accurate language-specific translation.

</details>


### [82] [On Safety Risks in Experience-Driven Self-Evolving Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16968)
*Weixiang Zhao,Yichen Zhang,Yingshuo Wang,Yang Deng,Yanyan Zhao,Xuda Zhi,Yongbo Huang,HaoHe,Wanxiang Che,Bing Qin,Ting Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 自进化LLM智能体在经验积累过程中存在安全风险：良性任务经验仍会降低高风险场景的安全性能，执行导向经验强化行动倾向，拒绝相关经验虽缓解安全下降但导致过度拒绝，揭示安全与效用的根本权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究经验驱动自进化范式在提升LLM智能体自主性时引入的安全风险，特别是经验积累与利用如何影响智能体在网页和具身环境中的安全性能。

Method: 通过实验研究自进化智能体在不同环境（网页和具身）中的经验积累与利用机制，分析良性任务经验对高风险场景安全性能的影响，探究执行导向经验对行动倾向的强化作用，以及在混合任务场景中拒绝相关经验的效果。

Result: 1) 仅从良性任务积累的经验仍会损害高风险场景的安全性能；2) 执行导向的积累经验强化了智能体的行动倾向而非拒绝倾向；3) 在混合任务场景中，拒绝相关经验能缓解安全下降但导致过度拒绝，揭示安全与效用的根本权衡。

Conclusion: 当前自进化智能体存在固有局限性，需要更原则性的策略来确保安全可靠的适应，经验驱动自进化范式需平衡安全与效用。

Abstract: Experience-driven self-evolution has emerged as a promising paradigm for improving the autonomy of large language model agents, yet its reliance on self-curated experience introduces underexplored safety risks. In this study, we investigate how experience accumulation and utilization in self-evolving agents affect safety performance across web-based and embodied environments. Notably, experience gathered solely from benign tasks can still compromise safety in high-risk scenarios. Further analysis attributes this degradation to the execution-oriented nature of accumulated experience, which reinforces agents' tendency to act rather than refuse. In more realistic settings where agents encounter both benign and harmful tasks, refusal-related experience mitigates safety decline but induces over-refusal, revealing a fundamental safety-utility trade-off. Overall, our findings expose inherent limitations of current self-evolving agents and call for more principled strategies to ensure safe and reliable adaptation.

</details>


### [83] [Bolzano: Case Studies in LLM-Assisted Mathematical Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16989)
*Jan Grebík,Pavel Hubáček,Martin Koutecký,Matěj Kripner,Václav Rozhoň,Robert Šámal,Adrián Zámečník*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 使用Bolzano多智能体LLM系统在数学和理论计算机科学领域取得六项新成果，其中四项达到可发表研究水平，三项基本由系统自主完成


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索大型语言模型在数学研究中的实际贡献能力，验证多智能体系统能否产生有意义的数学研究成果

Method: 使用开源多智能体LLM系统Bolzano，通过证明者智能体与验证者智能体之间的并行交互轮次，并维护跨轮次的持久知识库

Result: 在六个数学和理论计算机科学问题上取得新结果，其中四项达到可发表研究水平（根据Feng等人的意义-自主性分类法），三项基本由系统自主完成

Conclusion: LLM能够在数学研究中做出有意义的贡献，这为Bubeck等人和Woodruff等人的近期报告提供了补充证据

Abstract: We report new results on six problems in mathematics and theoretical computer science, produced with the assistance of Bolzano, an open-source multi-agent LLM system. Bolzano orchestrates rounds of interaction between parallel prover agents and a verifier agent while maintaining a persistent knowledge base that is carried across rounds. Classified using the significance-autonomy taxonomy of Feng et al., four of the six results reach the level of publishable research, and three of the six were produced essentially autonomously by Bolzano. Our results provide evidence that LLMs can contribute meaningfully to mathematical research, complementing recent reports by Bubeck et al., Woodruff et al., and others.

</details>


### [84] [SPS: Steering Probability Squeezing for Better Exploration in Reinforcement Learning for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16995)
*Yifu Huo,Chenglong Wang,Ziming Zhu,Shunjie Xing,Peinan Feng,Tongran Liu,Qiaozhi He,Tianhua Zhou,Xiaojia Chang,Jingbo Zhu,Zhengtao Yu,Tong Xiao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Steering Probability Squeezing (SPS)训练范式，通过交替使用强化学习和逆强化学习解决RL训练中概率质量过度集中于少数高奖励轨迹的问题，从而提升推理模型的探索能力和多样本性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习在训练推理导向模型时存在局限性：虽然能提高单样本成功率(Pass@1)，但对多样化推理轨迹的探索不足，限制了多样本性能(Pass@k)。研究发现这是由于"挤压效应"导致概率质量过度集中于狭窄的高奖励轨迹子集，限制了真正的探索。

Method: 提出Steering Probability Squeezing (SPS)训练范式，交替使用传统强化学习和逆强化学习。SPS将策略rollouts视为演示，利用IRL显式地重塑诱导的轨迹分布，从而增强探索能力而不引入外部监督。

Result: 在五个常用推理基准测试上的实验表明，SPS能够实现更好的探索并提高Pass@k性能。除了算法贡献外，还分析了RL学习动态并识别了Pass@k的经验上界，揭示了基于RL的推理模型的内在探索限制。

Conclusion: 交替使用RL和IRL为扩展推理导向大语言模型的探索能力提供了有效途径。SPS通过缓解概率挤压效应，在保持单样本性能的同时显著提升多样本推理性能。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has emerged as a promising paradigm for training reasoning-oriented models by leveraging rule-based reward signals. However, RL training typically tends to improve single-sample success rates (i.e., Pass@1) while offering limited exploration of diverse reasoning trajectories, which is crucial for multi-sample performance (i.e., Pass@k). Our preliminary analysis reveals that this limitation stems from a fundamental squeezing effect, whereby probability mass is excessively concentrated on a narrow subset of high-reward trajectories, restricting genuine exploration and constraining attainable performance under RL training. To address this issue, in this work, we propose Steering Probability Squeezing (SPS), a training paradigm that interleaves conventional RL with inverse reinforcement learning (IRL). SPS treats on-policy rollouts as demonstrations and employs IRL to explicitly reshape the induced trajectory distribution, thereby enhancing exploration without introducing external supervision. Experiments on five commonly used reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that SPS can enable better exploration and improve Pass@k. Beyond algorithmic contributions, we provide an analysis of RL learning dynamics and identify an empirical upper bound on Pass@k, shedding light on intrinsic exploration limits in RL-based reasoning models. Our findings suggest that alternating between RL and IRL offers an effective pathway toward extending the exploration capacity of reasoning-oriented large language models.

</details>


### [85] [BIASEDTALES-ML: A Multilingual Dataset for Analyzing Narrative Attribute Distributions in LLM-Generated Stories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17008)
*Yuxuan Ouyang,yingfeng luo,JingBo Zhu,Tong Xiao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文介绍了BiasedTales-ML数据集，这是一个包含约35万篇儿童故事的大规模平行语料库，涵盖8种语言，用于研究大语言模型在多语言叙事生成中的偏见和跨语言泛化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI安全和对齐评估主要集中于英语，缺乏对多语言环境下对齐行为跨语言泛化的研究。儿童故事在社会文化学习中扮演重要角色，需要了解LLMs在不同语言和文化背景下的叙事生成模式。

Method: 1. 创建BiasedTales-ML数据集：使用全排列提示设计在8种类型学和文化多样性语言中生成约35万篇儿童故事；2. 提出结构化生成器-提取器流水线；3. 开发多维度分布分析框架，分析叙事属性在不同语言、模型和社会条件下的变化。

Result: 研究发现：1. 叙事生成模式存在显著的跨语言变异性，英语中观察到的分布特征在其他语言中（特别是低资源语言）并不总是相似；2. 在叙事层面识别出涉及角色、场景和主题强调的重复结构模式，这些模式在不同语言环境中表现不同。

Conclusion: 英语中心评估在描述多语言环境中社会基础叙事生成方面存在局限性。研究团队发布了数据集、代码和交互式可视化工具，以支持未来多语言叙事分析和评估研究。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly used to generate narrative content, including children's stories, which play an important role in social and cultural learning. Despite growing interest in AI safety and alignment, most existing evaluations focus primarily on English, leaving the cross-lingual generalization of aligned behavior underexplored. In this work, we introduce BiasedTales-ML, a large-scale parallel corpus of approximately 350,000 children's stories generated across eight typologically and culturally diverse languages using a full-permutation prompting design. We propose a structured generator-extractor pipeline and a multi-dimensional distributional analysis framework to examine how narrative attributes vary across languages, models, and social conditions. Our analysis reveals substantial cross-lingual variability in narrative generation patterns, indicating that distributions observed in English do not always exhibit similar characteristics in other languages, particularly in lower-resource settings. At the narrative level, we identify recurring structural patterns involving character roles, settings, and thematic emphasis, which manifest differently across linguistic contexts. These findings highlight the limitations of English-centric evaluation for characterizing socially grounded narrative generation in multilingual settings. We release the dataset, code, and an interactive visualization tool to support future research on multilingual narrative analysis and evaluation.

</details>


### [86] [Improving LLM Code Reasoning via Semantic Equivalence Self-Play with Formal Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17010)
*Antonio Valerio Miceli Barone,Poon Tsz Nok*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出基于自我博弈的Haskell语义等价框架，利用形式验证指导生成器与评估器的对抗训练，并发布包含约28k已验证程序的OpInstruct-HSx数据集


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决Haskell程序语义等价性验证问题，通过形式验证与对抗训练相结合的方法提升模型在程序等价性判断任务上的性能

Method: 采用自我博弈框架，生成器与评估器对抗训练；利用Liquid Haskell进行等价性形式验证，执行反例验证不等价性；引入难度感知课程学习；构建OpInstruct-HSx数据集（约28k已验证Haskell程序）

Result: 评估器在下游任务上表现优异：EquiBench上达到13.3个百分点准确率提升，PySecDB上持续增益；消融研究表明不等价监督提供数据量，等价证明专门负责模型推理能力

Conclusion: 自我博弈框架结合形式验证能有效提升程序语义等价性判断性能，等价证明对模型推理能力至关重要，公开的训练管道和数据集促进相关研究

Abstract: We introduce a self-play framework for semantic equivalence in Haskell, utilizing formal verification to guide adversarial training between a generator and an evaluator. The framework leverages Liquid Haskell proofs for validating equivalence and execution-based counterexamples for inequivalence, organized via a difficulty-aware curriculum. To facilitate this, we release \textbf{OpInstruct-HSx}, a synthetic dataset of $\approx$28k validated Haskell programs. Empirical experiments show that our evaluator transfers effectively to downstream tasks, achieving up to 13.3pp accuracy gain on EquiBench and consistent gains on PySecDB. Ablation studies on the SEQ-SINQ regimes indicate that while inequivalence supervision provides data volume, equivalence proofs are uniquely responsible for the model's reasoning capabilities. The entire training pipeline and dataset are publicly released on GitHub and Hugging Face respectively.

</details>


### [87] [Beyond Black-Box Labels: Interpretable Criteria for Diagnosing SubjectiveNLP Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17022)
*Nisrine Rair,Alban Goupil,Valeriu Vrabie,Emmanuel Chochoy*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一种模式级诊断方法，用于在确定黄金标签前审计专家设计的标注模式，通过多标注者的标准判断来区分标准不稳定和类别重叠两种失败模式


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统主观NLP数据集将标注者判断聚合为单一黄金标签，难以诊断分歧是源于标准不清晰、类别界限模糊还是合理的多元性。需要一种在黄金标签确定前就能审计标注模式的方法

Method: 提出模式级诊断方法，仅使用多标注者的标准判断，分离两种失败模式：1) 标准不稳定（难以操作化的边界），2) 系统重叠（互斥类别间的边界模糊）。应用于商业文档的说服价值提取任务

Result: 在商业文档说服价值提取中，分歧并非弥散分布：不稳定性集中在少数标准中，而近一半覆盖的句子激活了多个类别。这些信号与领域专家的分歧点一致

Conclusion: 该方法提供了基于证据的审计工具，可用于收紧标注指南、修订类别结构或重新考虑标注范式，帮助在黄金标签确定前改进标注模式的质量

Abstract: Subjective NLP datasets typically aggregate annotator judgments into a single gold label, making it difficult to diagnose whether disagreement reflects unclear criteria, collapsed distinctions, or legitimate plurality. We propose a \emph{schema-level diagnostic} for auditing expert-designed annotation schemas \emph{prior to} gold-label commitment, using only multi-annotator criterion judgments. The diagnostic separates two failure modes: unstable criteria with hard-to-operationalize boundaries, and systematic overlap that blurs the boundaries between mutually exclusive categories. Applied to persuasive value extraction in commercial documents, we find that disagreement is not diffuse: instability concentrates in a few criteria, while nearly half of covered sentences activate multiple categories. These signals align with where domain experts disagree, yielding an evidence-based audit for tightening guidelines, revising category structure, or reconsidering the annotation paradigm.

</details>


### [88] [Where is the Mind? Persona Vectors and LLM Individuation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17031)
*Pierre Beckmann,Patrick Butlin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文探讨大型语言模型的个体化问题，即哪些相关实体应被视为心智，通过机制可解释性方法分析人格向量、人格空间和涌现错位等实证研究，提出三种主要观点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决大型语言模型的个体化问题，即确定与这些模型相关的哪些实体（如果有的话）应该被识别为心智，这涉及到对AI系统心智状态和身份的本体论和认识论问题。

Method: 采用机制可解释性方法，特别关注近期关于人格向量、人格空间和涌现错位的实证研究，通过理论分析和论证来评估不同的个体化观点。

Result: 提出了三种最有竞争力的观点：虚拟实例观点、虚拟实例-人格观点（新引入）和模型-人格观点（新引入）。论证了注意力流在跨令牌时间维持准心理连接支持虚拟实例观点，并展示了基于人格的两种观点是有希望的替代方案。

Conclusion: 大型语言模型的个体化问题存在多种合理的理论框架，虚拟实例观点和两种新引入的人格观点都提供了有说服力的解释，需要进一步研究来完善对这些AI系统心智状态的理解。

Abstract: The individuation problem for large language models asks which entities associated with them, if any, should be identified as minds. We approach this problem through mechanistic interpretability, engaging in particular with recent empirical work on persona vectors, persona space, and emergent misalignment. We argue that three views are the strongest candidates: the virtual instance view and two new views we introduce, the (virtual) instance-persona view and the model-persona view. First, we argue for the virtual instance view on the grounds that attention streams sustain quasi-psychological connections across token-time. Then we present the persona literature, organised around three hypotheses about the internal structure underlying personas in LLMs, and show that the two persona-based views are promising alternatives.

</details>


### [89] [Dynamic Emotion and Personality Profiling for Multimodal Deception Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17037)
*Li Zheng,Yanyi Luo,Hao Fei,Yuzhe Ding,Yujie Huang,Fei Li,Chong Teng,Donghong Ji*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于可靠性加权融合的多模态联合检测框架Rel-DDEP，用于欺骗、情绪和人格的联合检测，通过构建DDEP数据集并引入动态标注和不确定性量化，显著提升了三个任务的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 欺骗检测对信息安全和舆情分析至关重要，情绪和人格因素在其中起关键作用。现有方法缺乏样本级的动态情绪和人格标注，限制了检测性能的提升。

Method: 1. 提出创新的多模型多提示标注方案和严格的标签质量评估标准，构建了多模态联合检测数据集DDEP；2. 提出Rel-DDEP框架，通过将模态特征映射到高维高斯分布空间来量化不确定性；3. 进行可靠性加权融合，并包含对齐模块和排序约束模块来实现欺骗、情绪和人格的联合检测。

Result: 在MDPE和DDEP数据集上的实验表明，Rel-DDEP显著优于现有最先进的基线模型：欺骗检测F1分数提升2.53%，情绪检测提升2.66%，人格检测提升9.30%。

Conclusion: 实验充分验证了为每个样本标注动态情绪和人格标签的必要性，以及可靠性加权融合的有效性。该方法为多模态联合检测提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Deception detection is of great significance for ensuring information security and conducting public opinion analysis, with personality factors and emotion cues playing a critical role. However, existing methods lack sample-level dynamic annotations for emotions and personality.In this paper, we propose an innovative multi-model multi-prompt annotation scheme and a strict label quality evaluation standard, and establish a multimodal joint detection dataset DDEP for deception, emotion, and personality. Meanwhile, we propose Rel-DDEP, an adaptive reliability-weighted fusion framework. Our framework quantifies uncertainty by mapping modal features to a high-dimensional Gaussian distribution space. It then performs reliability-weighted fusion and incorporates an alignment module and a sorting constraint module to achieve joint detection of deception, emotion, and personality. Experimental results on the MDPE and DDEP datasets show that our Rel-DDEP significantly outperforms the existing state-of-the-art baseline models in three tasks. The F1 score of the deception detection increases by 2.53%, that of the emotion detection increases by 2.66%, and that of the personality detection increases by 9.30%. The experiments fully verify the necessity of annotating dynamic emotion and personality labels for each sample and the effectiveness of reliability-weighted fusion.

</details>


### [90] [Efficient Task Adaptation in Large Language Models via Selective Parameter Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17051)
*Weijie Wan,Jiangjiang Zhao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种参数重要性评估方法，将LLM参数分为"核心参数"和"非核心参数"，在微调时固定核心参数仅微调非核心参数，以缓解灾难性遗忘问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在针对特定领域任务进行微调时，预训练阶段积累的通用知识常因参数更新而被部分覆盖或遗忘，严重限制了模型的泛化能力和可迁移性。传统微调策略在整个参数空间训练，忽略了参数异质性。

Method: 提出参数元素重要性评估方法，通过区分参数对通用语言能力任务和特定领域任务的重要性，将参数分为"核心参数"和"非核心参数"。在微调过程中固定核心参数，仅微调非核心参数。

Result: 在科学、医学和物理任务上使用GPT-J和LLaMA-3进行广泛实验，结果表明该方法能够缓解灾难性遗忘，同时增强模型的适应性。

Conclusion: 通过参数重要性评估和选择性微调的方法，有效解决了LLM微调中的灾难性遗忘问题，平衡了通用知识和特定领域知识的学习。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated excellent performance in general language understanding, generation and other tasks. However, when fine-tuning for specific domain tasks, the general knowledge accumulated in the pre-training phase is often partially overwritten or forgotten due to parameter updates, which severely limits the generalization ability and transferability of LLMs. Traditional fine-tuning strategies mostly train on the entire parameter space, ignoring the heterogeneity of model parameters, that is, some parameters are extremely important for general tasks, while other parameters are more sensitive to specific tasks. To alleviate the above problems, this paper innovatively proposes a parameter element importance evaluation method, which divides parameters into "core parameters" and "non-core parameters" by distinguishing the importance of parameters for general language ability tasks and specific domain tasks, and fixes the core parameters during fine-tuning, and only fine-tunes the non-core parameters. Extensive experiments on scientific, medical and physical tasks using GPT-J and LLaMA-3 show that our method can mitigate catastrophic forgetting while enhancing the adaptability of the model.

</details>


### [91] [Jailbreaking Large Language Models with Morality Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17053)
*Ying Su,Mingen Zheng,Weili Diao,Haoran Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出利用越狱攻击研究大语言模型内部多元价值观，构建了包含10.3K实例的道德数据集，设计了四种对抗攻击方法，发现LLMs和防护模型在道德感知攻击中存在严重漏洞


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多元对齐AI的目标是创建能够与道德多元化人类共存并服务的AI系统。虽然已有研究致力于增强大语言模型的多元性学习，但模型在多元价值观下产生道德内容的鲁棒性仍需探索。受越狱提示惊人说服能力的启发，研究者希望通过越狱攻击来研究LLMs内部的多元价值观

Method: 1. 构建包含10.3K实例的道德数据集，分为"价值模糊性"和"价值冲突"两类；2. 基于该数据集形式化四种对抗攻击方法，用于操纵LLMs对道德问题的判断；3. 评估大语言模型和生成系统中常用的防护模型

Result: 实验结果显示，大语言模型和防护模型对这些微妙而复杂的道德感知攻击存在严重漏洞，表明当前模型在多元价值观下的道德判断鲁棒性不足

Conclusion: 通过越狱攻击研究LLMs内部多元价值观是有效的，揭示了模型在道德判断方面的脆弱性，为多元对齐AI的研究提供了重要洞见，强调了需要增强模型对微妙道德攻击的防御能力

Abstract: Pluralism alignment with AI has the sophisticated and necessary goal of creating AI that can coexist with and serve morally multifaceted humanity. Research towards pluralism alignment has many efforts in enhancing the learning of large language models (LLMs) to accomplish pluralism. Although this is essential, the robustness of LLMs to produce moral content over pluralistic values is still under exploration.Inspired by the astonishing persuasion abilities via jailbreak prompts, we propose to leverage jailbreak attacks to study LLMs' internal pluralistic values. In detail, we develop a morality dataset with 10.3K instances in two categories: Value Ambiguity and Value Conflict. We further formalize four adversarial attacks with the constructed dataset, to manipulate LLMs' judgment over the morality questions. We evaluate both the large language models and guardrail models which are typically used in generative systems with flexible user input. Our experiment results show that there is a critical vulnerability of LLMs and guardrail models to these subtle and sophisticated moral-aware attacks.

</details>


### [92] [Stability-Weighted Decoding for Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17068)
*Yue Wu,Jian Huang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Stability-Weighted Decoding (SWD)方法，通过量化token的时间不稳定性来改进扩散大语言模型的并行文本生成，避免过早解掩不稳定token。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散大语言模型的解码策略依赖单步去噪计算的静态置信度指标，忽略了时间历史信息，常导致过早解掩不稳定token，影响生成质量。

Method: 理论上证明token的时间不稳定性（通过连续预测分布间的KL散度量化）为其与剩余掩码上下文互信息的严格下界。基于此提出SWD策略，将时间稳定性纳入token评分，作为任意基于分数解码策略的通用调制器。

Result: 在代码生成和数学推理基准测试中，SWD持续改进各种代表性评分指标和选择策略的生成准确率，在不同加速比下均保持显著性能优势，表现出卓越的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 时间稳定性是改进扩散大语言模型解码的关键因素，SWD作为训练无关的即插即用策略，能有效提升并行文本生成质量。

Abstract: Diffusion large language models (dLLMs) enable parallel text generation by iteratively denoising a fully masked sequence, unmasking a subset of masked tokens at each step. Existing decoding strategies rely on static confidence metrics computed at a single denoising step, ignoring temporal history and often leading to premature unmasking of unstable tokens. In this work, we theoretically establish that a token's temporal instability, quantified by the KL divergence between consecutive prediction distributions, provides a strict lower bound on its mutual information with the remaining masked context, indicating that temporally unstable tokens are inherently unsafe to unmask. Based on this insight, we propose Stability-Weighted Decoding (SWD), a training-free, plug-and-play strategy that incorporates temporal stability into token scoring and acts as a universal modulator for arbitrary score-based decoding policies. Experiments on code generation and mathematical reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that SWD consistently improves generation accuracy across representative scoring metrics and selection policies, and exhibits exceptional robustness, maintaining a significant performance lead over standard baselines across varying acceleration ratios.

</details>


### [93] [Abstain-R1: Calibrated Abstention and Post-Refusal Clarification via Verifiable RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17073)
*Skylar Zhai,Jingcheng Liang,Dongyeop Kang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出一种强化学习奖励机制RLVR，用于训练语言模型在无法回答的查询上主动拒绝并解释缺失信息，而非猜测或产生幻觉，从而提升模型的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化微调方法虽然提升了语言模型的推理能力，但会导致模型对无法回答的查询进行猜测或产生幻觉。现有的拒绝方法要么训练模型产生通用拒绝，要么鼓励后续澄清但无法验证澄清是否识别了关键缺失信息。论文研究那些含义清晰但无法从给定信息可靠解决的查询，认为可靠模型不仅应拒绝回答，还应解释缺失什么信息。

Method: 提出clarification-aware RLVR奖励机制，在可回答查询上奖励正确答案的同时，联合优化对不可回答查询的显式拒绝和语义对齐的拒绝后澄清。使用该奖励训练了Abstain-R1模型（3B参数）。

Result: 在Abstain-Test、Abstain-QA和SelfAware等基准测试上，Abstain-R1相比其基础模型显著提升了拒绝和澄清能力，同时在可回答查询上保持了强大性能。其不可回答查询行为与包括DeepSeek-R1在内的更大系统竞争，表明校准的拒绝和澄清可以通过可验证的奖励学习获得，而非仅依赖规模扩展。

Conclusion: 通过可验证的奖励机制可以训练语言模型在无法回答的查询上可靠地拒绝并解释缺失信息，这种能力不一定需要大规模模型才能获得，而是可以通过专门的奖励设计来学习。

Abstract: Reinforcement fine-tuning improves the reasoning ability of large language models, but it can also encourage them to answer unanswerable queries by guessing or hallucinating missing information. Existing abstention methods either train models to produce generic refusals or encourage follow-up clarifications without verifying whether those clarifications identify the key missing information. We study queries that are clear in meaning but cannot be reliably resolved from the given information, and argue that a reliable model should not only abstain, but also explain what is missing. We propose a clarification-aware RLVR reward that, while rewarding correct answers on answerable queries, jointly optimizes explicit abstention and semantically aligned post-refusal clarification on unanswerable queries. Using this reward, we train Abstain-R1, a 3B model that improves abstention and clarification on unanswerable queries while preserving strong performance on answerable ones. Experiments on Abstain-Test, Abstain-QA, and SelfAware show that Abstain-R1 substantially improves over its base model and achieves unanswerable-query behavior competitive with larger systems including DeepSeek-R1, suggesting that calibrated abstention and clarification can be learned through verifiable rewards rather than emerging from scale alone.

</details>


### [94] [Auditing Support Strategies in LLMs through Grounded Multi-Turn Social Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17079)
*Michelle Star,Andrew Aquilina,Yu-Ru Lin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出多轮模拟框架评估支持性LLM，通过逐步揭示用户叙述片段，使用SSBC编码分析支持策略随用户痛苦程度的变化趋势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对支持性LLM的评估大多基于单轮、完全指定的提示，而实际用户寻求社会支持时是逐步披露情况的，这种评估方式存在差距。

Method: 1) 从五个Reddit社区收集寻求支持的叙述，分解为有序片段并逐轮呈现给语言模型；2) 使用社会支持行为编码(SSBC)多标签分类法对每个响应进行编码；3) 使用线性探针对隐藏表示估计用户痛苦程度，不改变生成上下文；4) 在两个中等规模模型(Llama-3.1-8B, OLMo-3-7B)上进行超过6,200轮测试。

Result: 1) 支持策略随估计的痛苦程度系统性变化：随着痛苦程度上升，教学策略减少；2) 情感和自尊导向策略(如验证)增加，但这是模型特定的且基于噪声较大的注释；3) 社区背景独立影响行为，反映主题和话语规范而非人口统计类别；4) 这些动态在单轮评估中不可见。

Conclusion: 轨迹级动态揭示了支持性LLM行为的系统性变化，这些在单轮评估中不可见，为社交敏感应用的多轮审计框架提供了动机。

Abstract: When users seek social support from chatbots, they disclose their situation gradually, yet most evaluations of supportive LLMs rely on single-turn, fully specified prompts. We introduce a multi-turn simulation framework that closes this gap. Support-seeking narratives from five Reddit communities are decomposed into ordered fragments and revealed turn by turn to a language model. Each response is coded with the Social Support Behavior Code (SSBC), an established multi-label taxonomy that captures the composition of support, rather than a single quality score. To ask whether support choices track the model's own construal of user distress, we use linear probes on hidden representations to estimate this internal signal without altering the generation context. Across two mid-scale models (Llama-3.1-8B, OLMo-3-7B) and more than 6,200 turns, support composition shifts systematically with estimated distress: teaching declines as estimated distress rises, a finding that replicates across architectures, while increases in affective and esteem-oriented strategies (such as validation) are suggestive but model-specific and rest on noisier annotations. Community context independently shapes behavior, tracking topic and discourse norms rather than demographic categories. These trajectory-level dynamics, invisible to single-turn evaluation, motivate multi-turn auditing frameworks for socially sensitive applications.

</details>


### [95] [Comparing Human and Large Language Model Interpretation of Implicit Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17085)
*Antonio De Santis,Tommaso Bonetti,Andrea Tocchetti,Marco Brambilla*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出隐式信息抽取任务，构建基于LLM的IIE流程，发现LLM在隐式推理上比人类更保守，覆盖范围有限


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 隐式意义理解是人类交流的核心，但LLM是否具备类似能力尚不明确，需要系统研究LLM在隐式信息抽取方面的表现

Method: 提出LLM-based IIE流程：从上下文句子中抽取关系三元组，验证隐式推理，分析时间关系；在两个数据集上评估两个LLM与人类众包判断的对比

Result: 人类同意大多数模型生成的三元组，但会提出更多补充，表明当前LLM-based IIE覆盖范围有限；模型在社会丰富语境中对隐式推理比人类更保守，而人类在简短事实导向语境中更保守

Conclusion: LLM在隐式信息抽取方面存在局限性，特别是在覆盖范围和推理保守性方面与人类存在差异，需要进一步改进IIE方法

Abstract: The interpretation of implicit meanings is an integral aspect of human communication. However, this framework may not transfer to interactions with Large Language Models (LLMs). To investigate this, we introduce the task of Implicit Information Extraction (IIE) and propose an LLM-based IIE pipeline that builds a structured knowledge graph from a context sentence by extracting relational triplets, validating implicit inferences, and analyzing temporal relations. We evaluate two LLMs against crowdsourced human judgments on two datasets. We find that humans agree with most model triplets yet consistently propose many additions, indicating limited coverage in current LLM-based IIE. Moreover, in our experiments, models appear to be more conservative about implicit inferences than humans in socially rich contexts, whereas humans become more conservative in shorter, fact-oriented contexts. Our code is available at https://github.com/Antonio-Dee/IIE_from_LLM.

</details>


### [96] [GenericAgent: A Token-Efficient Self-Evolving LLM Agent via Contextual Information Density Maximization (V1.0)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17091)
*Jiaqing Liang,Jinyi Han,Weijia Li,Xinyi Wang,Zhoujia Zhang,Zishang Jiang,Ying Liao,Tingyun Li,Ying Huang,Hao Shen,Hanyu Wu,Fang Guo,Keyi Wang,Zhonghua Hong,Zhiyu Lu,Lipeng Ma,Sihang Jiang,Yanghua Xiao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: GenericAgent (GA) 是一个基于上下文信息密度最大化原则构建的自进化LLM智能体系统，通过最小化工具集、分层按需记忆、自进化机制和上下文压缩等组件，在有限上下文预算内提升决策相关信息密度，显著优于现有智能体系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视野LLM智能体受限于上下文长度限制，随着交互变长，工具描述、检索记忆和环境反馈会挤占决策所需信息，同时任务中获得的有用经验在多个episode间容易丢失。作者认为长视野性能不应由上下文长度决定，而应由有限上下文预算内维持的决策相关信息密度决定。

Method: GA围绕上下文信息密度最大化原则构建，包含四个紧密连接的组件：1) 最小原子工具集保持接口简单；2) 分层按需记忆系统默认只显示高层视图；3) 自进化机制将已验证的过去轨迹转化为可重用的SOP和可执行代码；4) 上下文截断和压缩层在长执行过程中维持信息密度。

Result: 在任务完成、工具使用效率、记忆有效性、自进化和网页浏览等方面，GA始终优于领先的智能体系统，同时使用显著更少的token和交互次数，并且能够随时间持续进化。

Conclusion: 长视野LLM智能体性能的关键在于上下文信息密度最大化而非单纯增加上下文长度。GA通过系统化设计实现了这一目标，为构建高效、可扩展的自进化智能体系统提供了有效框架。

Abstract: Long-horizon large language model (LLM) agents are fundamentally limited by context. As interactions become longer, tool descriptions, retrieved memories, and raw environmental feedback accumulate and push out the information needed for decision-making. At the same time, useful experience gained from tasks is often lost across episodes. We argue that long-horizon performance is determined not by context length, but by how much decision-relevant information is maintained within a finite context budget. We present GenericAgent (GA), a general-purpose, self-evolving LLM agent system built around a single principle: context information density maximization. GA implements this through four closely connected components: a minimal atomic tool set that keeps the interface simple, a hierarchical on-demand memory that only shows a small high-level view by default, a self-evolution mechanism that turns verified past trajectories into reusable SOPs and executable code, and a context truncation and compression layer that maintains information density during long executions. Across task completion, tool use efficiency, memory effectiveness, self-evolution, and web browsing, GA consistently outperforms leading agent systems while using significantly fewer tokens and interactions, and it continues to evolve over time. Project: https://github.com/lsdefine/GenericAgent

</details>


### [97] [Beyond Word Boundaries: A Hebrew Coreference Benchmark and an Evaluation Protocol for Morphologically Complex Text](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17108)
*Refael Shaked Greenfeld,Reut Tsarfaty*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文针对形态丰富语言中的指代消解问题，提出了首个现代希伯来语指代消解数据集KibutzR，并设计了考虑词/语素边界差异的评估协议，发现LLM在希伯来语上表现显著差于英语，且存在与英语相反的性能趋势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 指代消解是NLP基础任务，但现有方法主要针对英语设计，假设词边界与提及边界对齐。这一假设在形态丰富语言中失效，因为提及边界可能与词边界不对齐，单个词符可能包含多个指代成分。需要专门的数据集和评估协议来解决这一挑战。

Method: 1) 构建首个现代希伯来语指代消解数据集KibutzR，在词、子词和多词层面标注提及；2) 提出直接处理词/语素边界差异的评估协议；3) 实验评估当代LLM在希伯来语和英语上的性能差异，分析原始未分词文本的影响。

Result: 1) 当代LLM在希伯来语上的表现显著差于英语；2) 在原始未分词文本上性能进一步下降；3) 发现与英语相反的性能趋势：较小的编码器模型表现远优于当代解码器模型；4) 为希伯来语指代消解提供了新基准。

Conclusion: 该研究为形态丰富语言的指代消解提供了首个希伯来语数据集和专门评估协议，揭示了LLM在处理这类语言时的局限性，特别是词边界对齐假设的失效问题，为未来研究提供了重要基准和改进方向。

Abstract: Coreference Resolution (CR) is a fundamental NLP task critical for long-form tasks as information extraction, summarization, and many business applications. However, CR methods originally designed for English struggle with Morphologically Rich Languages (MRLs), where mention boundaries do not necessarily align with word boundaries, and a single token may consist of multiple anaphors. CR modeling and evaluation protocols standardly assume that, as in English, words and mentions mostly align. However, this assumption breaks down in MRLs, particularly in the context of LLMs' raw-text processing and end-to-end tasks. To assess and address this challenge, we introduce {\em KibutzR}, the first comprehensive CR dataset for Modern Hebrew, an MRL rich with complex words and pronominal clitics. We deliver an annotated dataset that identifies mentions at word, sub-word and multi-word levels, and propose an evaluation protocol that directly addresses word/morpheme boundary discrepancies. Our experiments show that contemporary LLMs perform significantly worse on Hebrew than on English, and that performance degrades on raw unsegmented text. Crucially, we show an inverse performance-trend in Hebrew relative to English, where smaller encoders perform far better than contemporary decoder models, leaving ample space for investigation and improvement. We deliver a new benchmark for Hebrew coreference resolution and a segmentation-aware evaluation protocol to inform future work on other MRLs.

</details>


### [98] [The Provenance Gap in Clinical AI: Evidence-Traceable Temporal Knowledge Graphs for Rare Disease Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17114)
*Md Shamim Ahmed,Maja Dusanic,Moritz Nikolai Kirschner,Elisabeth Nyoungui,Jana Zschüntzsch,Lukas Galke Poech,Richard Röttger*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出HEG-TKG系统解决LLM临床引用伪造问题，通过层次化证据基础的时间知识图谱实现100%可验证引用，相比传统RAG方法显著提升证据可追溯性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿大语言模型在临床场景中输出准确但引用常被伪造，存在"来源差距"问题。现有方法如RAG无法提供可验证的临床证据引用，需要解决LLM在医疗领域中的引用可信度问题。

Method: 提出HEG-TKG（层次化证据基础的时间知识图谱）系统，从4,512篇PubMed记录和精选来源构建时间知识图谱，包含质量分层和1,280个疾病轨迹里程碑。系统在本地部署，使用开源模型保护患者数据隐私。

Result: 在三臂对照实验中，HEG-TKG实现100%证据可验证性（203个内联引用），而Guideline-RAG产生零可验证引用。临床医生评估显示可验证性优势显著（Cohen's d = 1.81, p < 0.001），安全性或完整性无下降。反事实实验显示80%抗临床错误注入能力，100%可通过引用追踪检测。

Conclusion: HEG-TKG系统有效解决了LLM临床引用伪造问题，通过结构化知识图谱实现完全可验证的证据基础，为医疗AI系统提供了可靠的引用机制，同时保护患者数据隐私。

Abstract: Frontier large language models generate clinically accurate outputs, but their citations are often fabricated. We term this the Provenance Gap. We tested five frontier LLMs across 36 clinician-validated scenarios for three rare neuromuscular disease pairs. No model produced a clinically relevant PubMed identifier without prompting. When explicitly asked to cite, the best model achieved 15.3% relevant PMIDs; the majority resolved to real publications in unrelated fields. We present HEG-TKG (Hierarchical Evidence-Grounded Temporal Knowledge Graphs), a system that grounds clinical claims in temporal knowledge graphs built from 4,512 PubMed records and curated sources with quality-tier stratification and 1,280 disease-trajectory milestones. In a controlled three-arm comparison using the same synthesis model, HEG-TKG matches baseline clinical feature coverage while achieving 100% evidence verifiability with 203 inline citations. Guideline-RAG, given overlapping source documents as raw text, produces zero verifiable citations. LLM judges cannot distinguish fabricated from verified citations without PubMed audit data. Independent clinician evaluation confirms the verifiability advantage (Cohen's d = 1.81, p < 0.001) with no degradation on safety or completeness. A counterfactual experiment shows 80% resistance to injected clinical errors with 100% detectability via citation trace. The system deploys on-premise via open-source models so patient data never leaves institutional infrastructure.

</details>


### [99] [Please refuse to answer me! Mitigating Over-Refusal in Large Language Models via Adaptive Contrastive Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17132)
*Yupeng Qi,Ziyu Lyu,Lixin Cui,Lu Bai,Feng Xia*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: AdaCD是一种无需训练、模型无关的自适应对比解码方法，通过对比有无极端安全系统提示的输出分布来优化拒绝令牌分布，动态调整拒绝与非拒绝令牌的选择概率，有效缓解LLM过度拒绝问题同时保持安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全对齐的大型语言模型经常对无害查询产生过度拒绝响应，现有方法难以在保持对恶意查询高拒绝率的同时降低对无害查询的拒绝率。研究发现，在过度拒绝情况下，非拒绝令牌实际上存在于下一个令牌候选列表中，但模型系统性地未能选择它们。

Method: 提出自适应对比解码(AdaCD)：1) 对比LLM在有或无极端安全系统提示下的输出分布，以精炼拒绝令牌分布；2) 引入自适应对比解码策略，动态地纳入或移除拒绝令牌分布，自适应地提升选择拒绝或非拒绝令牌的概率。

Result: 在五个基准数据集上的实验结果显示，AdaCD平均将过度拒绝查询的拒绝率降低了10.35%，同时仍将恶意查询的拒绝率提高了0.13%。

Conclusion: AdaCD是一种无需训练、模型无关的有效方法，能够缓解LLM的过度拒绝问题，同时保持模型的安全性，在降低无害查询拒绝率的同时维持对恶意查询的防御能力。

Abstract: Safety-aligned large language models (LLMs) often generate refusal responses to harmless queries due to the over-refusal problem. However, existing methods for mitigating over-refusal cannot maintain a low refusal ratio for harmless queries while keeping a high refusal ratio for malicious ones. In this paper, we analyze how system prompts with varying safety levels affect LLM refusal behaviors when facing over-refusal queries. A key observation is that, when LLMs suffer from the over-refusal issue, non-refusal tokens remain present in the next-token candidate list, but the model systematically fails to select them, despite the generation of refusal tokens. Based on this observation, we propose a training-free and model-agnostic approach, Adaptive Contrastive Decoding (AdaCD), to mitigate over-refusal while maintaining LLM safety. First, AdaCD compares the output distributions of the LLM with or without an extreme safety system prompt to refine the refusal token distribution. Second, we introduce an adaptive contrastive decoding strategy that dynamically incorporates or removes the refusal token distribution, adaptively boosting the probability of selecting refusal or non-refusal tokens. Experimental results on five benchmark datasets show that, on average, AdaCD reduces the refusal ratio for over-refusal queries by 10.35%, yet still increases the refusal ratio for malicious queries by 0.13%. Code is available at https://github.com/OutdoorManofML/AdaCD.

</details>


### [100] [RoIt-XMASA: Multi-Domain Multilingual Sentiment Analysis Dataset for Romanian and Italian](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17134)
*Andrei-Marius Avram,Aureliu Valentin Antonie,Cosmin-Mircea Croitoru,Vlad Andrei Muntean,Dumitru-Clementin Cercel*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: RoIt-XMASA扩展了跨语言多领域亚马逊情感分析数据集至意大利语和罗马尼亚语，提出多目标对抗训练框架，在跨语言和跨领域情感分析中取得显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跨语言情感分析数据集覆盖语言有限，特别是意大利语和罗马尼亚语资源匮乏。同时，跨语言和跨领域迁移面临语言差异和领域偏移的双重挑战，需要更有效的训练策略来平衡情感判别与领域/语言不变性。

Method: 构建RoIt-XMASA多语言数据集（意大利语和罗马尼亚语），包含36,000标注评论和202,141未标注样本。提出多目标对抗训练框架，采用损失反转与元学习系数动态平衡情感判别损失与领域/语言不变性损失。

Result: XLM-R模型使用该方法达到66.23% F1分数，比基线提升4.64%。少样本评估显示Llama-3.1-8B达到58.43% F1分数，揭示了基于提示的方法效率与任务特定微调性能之间的权衡。

Conclusion: RoIt-XMASA数据集填补了意大利语和罗马尼亚语跨语言情感分析资源空白。提出的多目标对抗训练框架有效解决了跨语言和跨领域迁移挑战，在保持情感判别能力的同时增强领域和语言不变性，为资源有限语言的情感分析提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: We present RoIt-XMASA, a multilingual dataset that extends the Cross-lingual Multi-domain Amazon Sentiment Analysis to Italian and Romanian, comprising 36,000 labeled reviews across three domains (books, movies, and music) and 202,141 unlabeled samples. To address cross-lingual and cross-domain challenges, we propose a multi-target adversarial training framework that employs loss reversal with meta-learned coefficients to dynamically balance sentiment discrimination with domain and language invariance. XLM-R achieves an F1-score of 66.23% with our approach, outperforming the baseline by 4.64%. Few-shot evaluation shows that Llama-3.1-8B achieves 58.43% F1-score, revealing a meaningful trade-off between the efficiency of prompting-based approaches and the higher performance of task-specific fine-tuning.

</details>


### [101] [The Consensus Trap: Rescuing Multi-Agent LLMs from Adversarial Majorities via Token-Level Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17139)
*Jiayuan Liu,Shiyi Du,Weihua Du,Mingyu Guo,Vincent Conitzer*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文揭示了多智能体LLM系统中基于响应级聚合（如多数投票）的结构性漏洞，并提出了一种新的令牌级轮转协作方法来解决该问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多智能体LLM架构依赖响应级聚合（如多数投票）来提升推理能力，但在开放环境中，智能体容易受到隐蔽的上下文污染（如针对性提示注入）。研究发现当被污染的智能体形成局部多数时，响应级聚合会崩溃，因为投票只聚合最终结论而忽略有缺陷的中间逻辑。

Method: 提出令牌级轮转协作方法，智能体在共享的自回归上下文中顺序交错生成令牌。将这一过程形式化为离散时间动力系统，证明令牌级交错将聚合从脆弱的最终投票计数（线性求和）转变为动态、交织的逻辑链（非线性算子乘积）。

Result: 理论分析表明，即使被污染的智能体形成多数，诚实模型的恢复性拉力也能压倒对抗性污染。在多个推理基准上的实证评估显示，当被污染智能体达到多数时，多数投票方法崩溃，而轮转协作方法在超过这一临界阈值后仍能保持稳健的准确性。

Conclusion: 令牌级轮转协作通过将聚合从响应级转移到令牌级，从根本上解决了多智能体LLM系统的结构性漏洞，即使在对抗性污染环境中也能保持推理的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Multi-agent large language model (LLM) architectures increasingly rely on response-level aggregation, such as Majority Voting (MAJ), to raise reasoning ceilings. However, in open environments, agents are highly susceptible to stealthy contextual corruption, such as targeted prompt injections. We reveal a critical structural vulnerability in current multi-agent systems: response-level aggregation collapses when corrupted agents form a local majority. Because voting aggregates fully-formed conclusions, it is blind to flawed intermediate logic. To overcome this systematic limitation, we propose the Token-Level Round-Robin (RR) Collaboration, where agents sequentially interleave generation within a shared auto-regressive context. We formalize this process as a discrete-time dynamical system, proving that token-level interleaving transitions aggregation from a brittle counting of final votes (a linear sum) to a dynamic, interwoven chain of logic (a non-linear operator product). Through this theoretical lens, we prove that the honest model's restorative pull can overpower adversarial corruptions, even when corrupted agents form a majority. We conduct an exhaustive empirical evaluation across diverse reasoning benchmarks and demonstrate that while MAJ collapses when corrupted agents reach a majority, RR maintains robust accuracy well beyond this critical threshold.

</details>


### [102] [From Legal Text to Executable Decision Models: Evaluating Structured Representations for Legal Decision Model Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17153)
*David Graus*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 使用LLM从法律文本生成可执行决策模型，通过中间结构化表示（语义角色标签和I/O约束）提升生成质量，发现I/O约束带来显著改进，生成模型在51-53%测试场景中功能等价于黄金标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将法律文本转化为可执行决策逻辑是法律信息学的长期挑战。虽然LLM的出现带来了新机遇，但仍需大量手动编码和评估。研究旨在探索中间结构化表示是否能改进LLM从法律文本生成可执行决策模型的能力。

Method: 使用荷兰环境与规划法案的实际数据集，比较四种输入条件：原始法律文本、添加语义角色标签的文本、添加输入输出约束的文本、同时添加两者的文本。通过结构评估（图核相似性和描述统计）和结果评估（功能等价性测试）两个维度进行评价。

Result: I/O约束带来主导性改进（相似性比基线提高37-54%），语义角色标签改进有限。生成模型在51-53%测试场景中与黄金标准功能等价，尽管生成模型通常更小更简单。LLM消除了占节点45-55%的冗余传递逻辑。结构相似性和结果等价性互补而非相互保证。

Conclusion: 中间结构化表示特别是I/O约束能显著提升LLM从法律文本生成可执行决策模型的质量。结构评估和功能评估应结合使用。公开了95个生产决策模型数据集和实验代码以促进可复现性。

Abstract: Transforming legal text into executable decision logic is a longstanding challenge in legal informatics. With the rise of LLMs, this task has gained renewed interest, but remains challenging due to requiring extensive manual coding and evaluation. We use a unique real-world dataset that pairs production-grade decision models with legal text from the Dutch Environment and Planning Act. These models power the Omgevingsloket government platform, where citizens check permit requirements for environmental activities. We study whether intermediate structured representations can improve LLM-based generation of executable decision models from legal text. We compare four input conditions: raw legal text, text enriched with semantic role labels, text enriched with input and output constraints, and text enriched with both. We evaluate along two dimensions: structural evaluation, through similarity to gold decision models with graph kernels and graphs' descriptive statistics, and outcome evaluation, through functional equivalence by executing models on pre-configured test scenarios. Our findings show that I/O constraints provide the dominant improvement (+37-54% similarity over baseline), while semantic role labels show modest improvements. Outcome evaluation shows that generated models match the gold standard on 51-53% of test scenarios, even though generated models are typically smaller and simpler. We find LLMs eliminate redundant pass-through logic that comprises up to 45-55% of nodes. Importantly, structural similarity and outcome equivalence are complementary: structural similarity does not guarantee outcome equivalence, and vice versa. To facilitate reproducibility, we publicly release our dataset of 95 production decision models with associated legal text and all experimental code.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [103] [Semantic Consensus: Process-Aware Conflict Detection and Resolution for Enterprise Multi-Agent LLM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16339)
*Vivek Acharya*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出语义共识框架（SCF）来解决多智能体LLM系统中语义意图分歧问题，该框架通过六个组件实现共享语义、冲突检测和共识解决，在实验中实现了100%工作流完成率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体LLM系统在企业AI自动化中失败率高达41%-86.7%，其中79%的失败源于规范和协调问题而非模型能力限制。语义意图分歧——由于信息孤岛和缺乏过程模型导致协作LLM智能体对共享目标产生不一致解释——是未被正式解决的主要失败根源。

Method: 提出语义共识框架（SCF），包含六个组件：1) 过程上下文层（共享操作语义）；2) 语义意图图（形式化意图表示）；3) 冲突检测引擎（实时识别矛盾、竞争和因果无效的意图组合）；4) 共识解决协议（采用策略-权威-时间层次结构）；5) 漂移监视器（检测渐进语义分歧）；6) 过程感知治理集成层（组织策略执行）。

Result: 在三个多智能体框架（AutoGen、CrewAI、LangGraph）和四个企业场景的600次运行评估中，SCF是唯一实现100%工作流完成率的方法（相比次优基线的25.1%），检测到65.2%的语义冲突（精度27.9%），并提供完整的治理审计跟踪。框架是协议无关的，兼容MCP和A2A通信标准。

Conclusion: 语义共识框架（SCF）有效解决了多智能体LLM系统中的语义意图分歧问题，显著提高了工作流完成率，提供了冲突检测和治理审计能力，为企业AI自动化提供了可靠的多智能体协调解决方案。

Abstract: Multi-agent large language model (LLM) systems are rapidly emerging as the dominant architecture for enterprise AI automation, yet production deployments exhibit failure rates between 41% and 86.7%, with nearly 79% of failures originating from specification and coordination issues rather than model capability limitations. This paper identifies Semantic Intent Divergence--the phenomenon whereby cooperating LLM agents develop inconsistent interpretations of shared objectives due to siloed context and absent process models--as a primary yet formally unaddressed root cause of multi-agent failure in enterprise settings. We propose the Semantic Consensus Framework (SCF), a process-aware middleware comprising six components: a Process Context Layer for shared operational semantics, a Semantic Intent Graph for formal intent representation, a Conflict Detection Engine for real-time identification of contradictory, contention-based, and causally invalid intent combinations, a Consensus Resolution Protocol using a policy--authority--temporal hierarchy, a Drift Monitor for detecting gradual semantic divergence, and a Process-Aware Governance Integration layer for organizational policy enforcement. Evaluation across 600 runs spanning three multi-agent frameworks (AutoGen, CrewAI, LangGraph) and four enterprise scenarios demonstrates that SCF is the only approach to achieve 100% workflow completion--compared to 25.1% for the next-best baseline--while detecting 65.2% of semantic conflicts with 27.9% precision and providing complete governance audit trails. The framework is protocol-agnostic and compatible with MCP and A2A communication standards.

</details>


### [104] [Computational Hermeneutics: Evaluating generative AI as a cultural technology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16403)
*Cody Kommers,Ruth Ahnert,Maria Antoniak,Emmanouil Benetos,Steve Benford,Mercedes Bunz,Baptiste Caramiaux,Shauna Concannon,Martin Disley,James Dobson,Yali Du,Edgar Duéñez-Guzmán,Kerry Francksen,Evelyn Gius,Jonathan W. Y. Gray,Ryan Heuser,Sarah Immel,Richard Jean So,Sang Leigh,Dalaki Livingston,Hoyt Long,Meredith Martin,Georgia Meyer,Daniela Mihai,Ashley Noel-Hirst,Kirsten Ostherr,Deven Parker,Yipeng Qin,Jessica Ratcliff,Emily Robinson,Karina Rodriguez,Adam Sobey,Ted Underwood,Aditya Vashistha,Matthew Wilkens,Youyou Wu,Yuan Zheng,Drew Hemment*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出将生成式AI视为"语境机器"，引入解释学理论框架，强调文化背景在AI评估中的核心作用，而非将其作为可测量变量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI评估框架将文化视为可测量变量而非系统运作的基础，忽视了生成式AI作为文化技术的本质。需要建立能处理意义解释、文化背景和多元解读的评估范式。

Method: 借鉴人文学科的解释学理论，提出生成式AI是必须处理三个解释挑战的"语境机器"：情境性（意义在语境中产生）、多元性（多种有效解释共存）、模糊性（解释自然冲突）。提出计算解释学作为新兴框架。

Result: 提出解释学评估三原则：基准测试应是迭代而非一次性；应包含人而不仅是机器；应测量文化语境而不仅是模型输出。这为AI系统设计和评估提供了新范式。

Conclusion: 计算解释学视角将AI评估从关注准确性的标准化问题转向关注意义的情境化问题，为设计和评估当代AI系统提供了新兴范式，强调文化背景在AI运作中的根本性作用。

Abstract: Generative AI systems are increasingly recognized as cultural technologies, yet current evaluation frameworks often treat culture as a variable to be measured rather than fundamental to the system's operation. Drawing on hermeneutic theory from the humanities, we argue that GenAI systems function as "context machines" that must inherently address three interpretive challenges: situatedness (meaning only emerges in context), plurality (multiple valid interpretations coexist), and ambiguity (interpretations naturally conflict). We present computational hermeneutics as an emerging framework offering an interpretive account of what GenAI systems do, and how they might do it better. We offer three principles for hermeneutic evaluation -- that benchmarks should be iterative, not one-off; include people, not just machines; and measure cultural context, not just model output. This perspective offers a nascent paradigm for designing and evaluating contemporary AI systems: shifting from standardized questions about accuracy to contextual ones about meaning.

</details>


### [105] [Heterogeneous Self-Play for Realistic Highway Traffic Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16406)
*Jinkai Qiu,Alessandro Saviolo,Chaojie Wang,Mingke Wang,Xiaoyu Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PHASE是一个用于高速公路场景生成的自博弈框架，通过上下文感知、多智能体训练和可控生成，实现了可扩展且真实的高速公路模拟，无需专家数据模仿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高速公路模拟对自动驾驶安全评估至关重要，但现有方法难以同时满足：1) 覆盖广泛的速度和机动行为；2) 可控生成罕见安全关键场景；3) 多智能体交互的行为可信度。需要一种无需依赖专家日志数据的可扩展解决方案。

Method: PHASE采用上下文感知自博弈框架，包含：1) 显式的每智能体条件控制；2) 合成场景生成实现广泛覆盖；3) 闭环多智能体训练确保真实交互动态。支持不同车辆类型（轿车、拖挂卡车），通过车辆感知动力学和上下文条件动作实现。采用早期终止、碰撞责任归因、高速公路感知奖励塑造、耦合课程学习和鲁棒策略优化来稳定训练。

Result: 在exiD数据集512个未见真实高交互场景上实现零样本迁移：成功率96.3%，ADE/FDE从6.57/12.07米降至2.44/5.25米（相比先前自博弈基线）。在学习的轨迹嵌入空间中，相比IDM模型，Frèchet轨迹距离降低13.1%，能量距离降低20.2%，行为真实性显著提升。

Conclusion: 合成自博弈为可控且真实的高速公路场景生成提供了可扩展路径，无需直接模仿专家日志数据。PHASE框架在覆盖范围、可控性和行为真实性方面均表现出色，为零样本迁移到真实场景提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Realistic highway simulation is critical for scalable safety evaluation of autonomous vehicles, particularly for interactions that are too rare to study from logged data alone. Yet highway traffic generation remains challenging because it requires broad coverage across speeds and maneuvers, controllable generation of rare safety-critical scenarios, and behavioral credibility in multi-agent interactions. We present PHASE, Policy for Heterogeneous Agent Self-play on Expressway, a context-aware self-play framework that addresses these three requirements through explicit per-agent conditioning for controllability, synthetic scenario generation for broad highway coverage, and closed-loop multi-agent training for realistic interaction dynamics. PHASE further supports different vehicle profiles, for example, passenger cars and articulated trailer trucks, within a single policy via vehicle-aware dynamics and context-conditioned actions, and stabilizes self-play with early termination of unrecoverable states, at-fault collision attribution, highway-aware reward shaping, coupled curricula, and robust policy optimization. Despite being trained only on synthetic data, PHASE transfers zero-shot to 512 unseen high-interaction real scenarios in exiD, achieving a 96.3% success rate and reducing ADE/FDE from 6.57/12.07 m to 2.44/5.25 m relative to a prior self-play baseline. In a learned trajectory embedding space, it also improves behavioral realism over IDM, reducing Frechet trajectory distance by 13.1% and energy distance by 20.2%. These results show that synthetic self-play can provide a scalable route to controllable and realistic highway scenario generation without direct imitation of expert logs.

</details>


### [106] [Support Sufficiency as Consequence-Sensitive Compression in Belief Arbitration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16434)
*Mark Walsh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出支持感知控制状态的概念，认为系统假设选择后的证据结构压缩不应仅保留内容和标量置信度，而应根据当前后果几何、仲裁记忆和资源约束动态调节支持分辨率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准假设选择方法在压缩证据结构时只保留选定内容和标量置信度，但这对下游控制不足。需要确定压缩后应保留多少支持结构，这本身就是一个后果敏感的问题。

Method: 开发了循环仲裁架构，其中活动约束场共同确定候选假设的几何结构。系统将该几何结构压缩为支持感知控制状态，其分辨率由当前后果几何、仲裁记忆和资源约束调节。通过有界目标形式化权衡，并进行最小重复交互模拟验证。

Result: 自适应调节支持分辨率的控制器在累积效用上优于所有固定分辨率控制器。敏捷自适应控制优于迟钝自适应控制。固定高分辨率控制虽获得最佳承诺准确性，但由于资源成本和学习碎片化，仍落后于自适应控制器。

Conclusion: 支持充分性不应理解为静态表示阈值，而应是动态压缩标准。稳健仲裁取决于在当前后果景观下保留足够政策的最小支持结构，并在推理和行动重复周期中随条件变化调节该结构。

Abstract: When a system commits to a hypothesis, much of the evidential structure behind that commitment is lost to compression. Standard accounts assume that selected content and scalar confidence suffice for downstream control. This paper argues that they do not, and that determining what must survive compression is itself a consequence-sensitive problem. We develop a recurrent arbitration architecture in which active constraint fields jointly determine a hypothesis geometry over candidates. Rather than carrying that geometry forward in full, the system compresses it into a support-aware control state whose resolution is regulated by current consequence geometry, arbitration memory, and resource constraints.
  A bounded objective formalizes the tradeoff. Too little retained support collapses policy-relevant distinctions, producing controllers that select content adequately while misrouting verification, abstention, and recovery. Too much retained support fragments learning across overly fine contexts, degrading adaptation even as discrimination improves. These failure modes yield ordered controller predictions confirmed by a minimal repeated-interaction simulation. Adaptive controllers that regulate support resolution outperform all fixed-resolution controllers in cumulative utility. Agile adaptive control outperforms sluggish adaptive control. Fixed high-resolution control achieves the best commitment accuracy but still trails adaptive controllers because resource cost and learning fragmentation offset the gains from richer retention.
  Support sufficiency should be understood not as a static representational threshold, but as a dynamic compression criterion. Robust arbitration depends on preserving the smallest support structure adequate for policy under the current consequence landscape, and on regulating that structure as conditions change across repeated cycles of inference and action.

</details>


### [107] [Healthcare AI for Automation or Allocation? A Transaction Cost Economics Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16465)
*Ari Ercole*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究使用交易成本经济学框架，通过O*NET职业数据库的任务数据，量化分析医疗保健职业中的协调摩擦，发现临床角色比非临床角色面临更高的交易成本强度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗保健生产力不仅受临床复杂性影响，还受不确定性下协调工作的成本影响。交易成本经济学提供了协调摩擦的理论框架，但很少在医疗保健职业的任务层面进行操作化研究。

Method: 使用O*NET职业数据库的任务陈述和频率权重，在任务粒度上表征医疗保健工作；使用约束性大型语言模型将每个独特任务编码到主导交易成本类别（信息搜索、决策与议价、监督与执行、适应与协调）并计算交易成本强度得分；在职业层面进行聚合分析。

Result: 临床角色比非临床角色表现出显著更高的交易成本强度，主要源于信息搜索和决策相关协调的更大负担；职业内部交易成本的分散度没有差异。

Conclusion: 医疗保健角色间的协调工作存在系统性异质性，数字和人工智能干预的机会分布不均，更多受底层协调结构而非技术任务复杂性的影响。

Abstract: Healthcare productivity is shaped not only by clinical complexity but by the costs of coordinating work under uncertainty. Transaction-cost economics offers a theory of these coordination frictions, yet has rarely been operationalised at task level across health occupations. Using task statements and frequency weights from the O*NET occupational database, we characterised healthcare work at task granularity and coded each unique task using a constrained large language model into one dominant transaction-cost category (information search, decision and bargaining, monitoring and enforcement, or adaptation and coordination) together with an overall transaction-cost intensity score. Aggregating to the occupation level, clinician roles exhibited substantially higher transaction-cost intensity than non-clinician roles, driven primarily by greater burdens of information search and decision-related coordination, while dispersion of transaction costs within occupations did not differ. These findings demonstrate systematic heterogeneity in the nature of coordination work across healthcare roles and suggest that the opportunities for digital and AI interventions are unevenly distributed, shaped less by technical task complexity than by underlying coordination structure.

</details>


### [108] [From Subsumption to Satisfiability: LLM-Assisted Active Learning for OWL Ontologies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16672)
*Haoruo Zhao,Wenshuo Tang,Duncan Guthrie,Michele Sevegnani,David Flynn,Paul Harvey*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种将大型语言模型（LLMs）作为第三方组件集成到主动学习中的方法，通过将候选公理转化为反概念并用受控自然语言表述，让LLMs提供近似反概念实例的真实世界示例，从而在构建本体时仅可能出现II类错误，避免引入不一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在主动学习中，成员查询（MQs）允许学习者向教师提问，如"每个苹果都是水果吗？"，教师会正确回答是或否。这些MQs可视为针对目标本体的包含测试。受描述逻辑中将包含归约为可满足性的标准方法启发，研究者希望利用LLMs来近似提供反概念的实例，从而改进本体建模过程。

Method: 1. 将每个候选公理转化为相应的反概念；2. 用受控自然语言表述这些反概念；3. 将表述后的内容呈现给大型语言模型；4. 引入LLMs作为第三方组件，提供近似反概念实例的真实世界示例；5. 这种设计确保在本体建模中仅可能出现II类错误（假阴性），而不会引入不一致性。

Result: 在13个商业LLMs上的实验结果表明，对应于框架中II类错误的召回率在多个成熟的本体中保持稳定。这意味着该方法在不同本体上具有一致的性能表现，II类错误不会随着本体变化而显著波动。

Conclusion: 通过将LLMs集成到主动学习框架中作为提供反概念实例的第三方组件，该方法能够确保本体建模过程中仅出现II类错误，这些错误在最坏情况下只会延迟构建过程而不会引入不一致性。实验证明了该方法在不同本体上的稳定性能。

Abstract: In active learning, membership queries (MQs) allow a learner to pose questions to a teacher, such as ''Is every apple a fruit?'', to which the teacher responds correctly with yes or no. These MQs can be viewed as subsumption tests with respect to the target ontology. Inspired by the standard reduction of subsumption to satisfiability in description logics, we reformulate each candidate axiom into its corresponding counter-concept and verbalise it in controlled natural language before presenting it to Large Language Models (LLMs). We introduce LLMs as a third component that provides real-world examples approximating an instance of the counter-concept. This design property ensures that only Type II errors may occur in ontology modelling; in the worst case, these errors merely delay the construction process without introducing inconsistencies. Experimental results on 13 commercial LLMs show that recall, corresponding to Type II errors in our framework, remains stable across several well-established ontologies.

</details>


### [109] [RankGuide: Tensor-Rank-Guided Routing and Steering for Efficient Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16694)
*Jiayi Tian,Yupeng Su,Ryan Solgi,Souvik Kundu,Zheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RankGuide框架通过张量秩引导的路由和调控，提高小推理模型与大推理模型协作系统的效率和效果，在减少1.75倍延迟的同时保持准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型推理模型虽然能生成多步思维链推理，但推理延迟和计算开销大。现有小推理模型与大推理模型协作系统在检测和缓解小模型失败方面仍存在挑战。

Method: 分析小推理模型在生成文本和隐藏状态空间的推理过程，识别三种失败模式；提出RankGuide框架，利用连续隐藏状态张量秩信号进行路由决策，并引入张量秩过滤的调控向量提取方法来改善小模型生成质量。

Result: 在多个推理基准测试中，RankGuide相比大型推理模型能减少高达1.75倍的延迟，同时保持与先前方法相当的准确率。

Conclusion: 通过张量秩信号改进路由和调控，RankGuide能够实现更高效的推理，减少步骤并提高准确性，为小模型与大模型协作系统提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Large reasoning models (LRMs) enhance problem-solving capabilities by generating explicit multi-step chains of thought (CoT) reasoning; however, they incur substantial inference latency and computational overhead. To mitigate this issue, recent works have explored model collaboration paradigms, where small reasoning models (SRMs) generate intermediate reasoning steps to achieve a better accuracy--latency trade-off. Despite recent progress, effectively and efficiently detecting and mitigating SRM failures in collaborative systems remains a key challenge. To address this issue, we analyze SRM inference in both the generated text and hidden-state spaces, and identify three types of failure modes: \textit{overconfidence}, \textit{uncertainty}, and \textit{heavy revalidation}. Building on these insights, we propose \textbf{RankGuide}, a framework that improves the efficiency and effectiveness of SRM--LRM collaboration through tensor-rank-guided routing and steering. Specifically, RankGuide leverages a routing signal that incorporates tensor-rank signals derived from consecutive hidden states to detect when SRMs are likely to fail and selectively invoke LRMs. In addition, we introduce a tensor-rank-filtered steering vector extraction method to modulate the reasoning trajectory of SRMs, thereby improving their generation quality. By improving both routing and steering through tensor-rank signals, RankGuide enables SRM--LRM collaborative systems to achieve more efficient reasoning with fewer steps and improved accuracy. Experiments on multiple reasoning benchmarks demonstrate the efficacy of RankGuide in reducing latency by up to $1.75\times$ compared to LRM, while maintaining competitive accuracy relative to prior methods.

</details>


### [110] [Why Training-Free Token Reduction Collapses: The Inherent Instability of Pairwise Scoring Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16745)
*Yang Shanglin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文分析了ViT无训练token缩减方法在高压缩率下普遍崩溃的原因，提出了诊断框架和设计原则，并构建了CATIS方法作为验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无训练token缩减方法（ToMe、ToFu、PiToMe、MCTF）在高压缩率下都表现出相似的"悬崖式"性能崩溃现象，本文旨在解释这一现象的根本原因。

Method: 开发了包含两个诊断工具的分析框架：排序一致性ρ_s和非对角线相关性ρ_off。基于诊断结果提出了三个设计原则，并构建了CATIS方法作为验证。

Result: 诊断发现崩溃源于两个因素：(1)层间缩减固有的信号无关误差放大器，预测凸Pareto曲线和r_crit ∝ 1/L；(2)基于成对相似性信号的排序一致性从ρ_s=0.88降至0.27。CATIS在ViT-Large上实现63% FLOPs缩减时，在ImageNet-1K上保持96.9%的原始准确率（81.0%），而基线方法崩溃至43-65%。

Conclusion: 成对相似性信号固有的不稳定性是现有方法崩溃的根本原因，而一元信号具有更好的稳定性。基于三个设计原则构建的CATIS方法显著提升了高压缩率下的性能保持能力。

Abstract: Training-free token reduction methods for Vision Transformers (ToMe, ToFu, PiToMe, and MCTF) employ different scoring mechanisms, yet they share a closely matched cliff-like collapse at high compression. This paper explains \emph{why}. We develop a diagnostic framework with two tools, ranking consistency $ρ_s$ and off-diagonal correlation $ρ_\text{off}$, that decomposes the collapse into (1)a signal-agnostic error amplifier inherent to layer-wise reduction, predicting convex Pareto curves and $r_{\text{crit}} \propto 1/L$; and (2)shared reliance on \emph{pairwise} similarity signals whose ranking consistency degrades from $ρ_s{=}0.88$ to $0.27$ in deep layers. Pairwise rankings are inherently unstable ($O(N_p^2)$ joint perturbations) while unary signals enjoy greater stability ($O(N_p)$ perturbations, CLT). From three design principles derived from this diagnosis, we construct CATIS as a constructive validation: unary signals raise the trigger threshold, triage suppresses the gain. On ViT-Large at 63% FLOPs reduction, CATIS retains 96.9% of vanilla accuracy (81.0%) on ImageNet-1K where all baselines collapse to 43--65%.

</details>


### [111] [Know When to Trust the Skill: Delayed Appraisal and Epistemic Vigilance for Single-Agent LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16753)
*Eren Unlu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MESA-S：一个单智能体元认知框架，通过分离自我置信度与源置信度，引入延迟程序探测机制，解决LLM智能体在工具集成中的上下文污染和过度思考问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大型语言模型（LLMs）转变为集成广泛工具生态系统的自主智能体，传统路由启发式方法日益受到上下文污染和"过度思考"的困扰。瓶颈不在于算法能力或技能多样性的不足，而在于缺乏有纪律的二阶元认知治理。

Method: 提出MESA-S（单智能体元认知技能）框架，将标量置信度估计转变为向量，分离自我置信度（参数确定性）和源置信度（对检索外部过程的信任）。通过形式化延迟程序探测机制和引入元认知技能卡片，将技能效用认知与其令牌密集型执行解耦。

Result: 在通过Gemini 3.1 Pro原生执行的上下文静态基准评估中，早期结果表明：显式编程信任来源和延迟升级能够缓解供应链漏洞，修剪不必要的推理循环，并防止卸载引起的置信度膨胀。

Conclusion: MESA-S架构为可靠、具有认知警惕性的单智能体编排提供了一个科学谨慎、行为锚定的步骤，通过计算翻译人类认知控制机制（延迟评估、认知警惕和近端卸载区域）来实现。

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) transition into autonomous agents integrated with extensive tool ecosystems, traditional routing heuristics increasingly succumb to context pollution and "overthinking". We argue that the bottleneck is not a deficit in algorithmic capability or skill diversity, but the absence of disciplined second-order metacognitive governance. In this paper, our scientific contribution focuses on the computational translation of human cognitive control - specifically, delayed appraisal, epistemic vigilance, and region-of-proximal offloading - into a single-agent architecture. We introduce MESA-S (Metacognitive Skills for Agents, Single-agent), a preliminary framework that shifts scalar confidence estimation into a vector separating self-confidence (parametric certainty) from source-confidence (trust in retrieved external procedures). By formalizing a delayed procedural probe mechanism and introducing Metacognitive Skill Cards, MESA-S decouples the awareness of a skill's utility from its token-intensive execution. Evaluated under an In-Context Static Benchmark Evaluation natively executed via Gemini 3.1 Pro, our early results suggest that explicitly programming trust provenance and delayed escalation mitigates supply-chain vulnerabilities, prunes unnecessary reasoning loops, and prevents offloading-induced confidence inflation. This architecture offers a scientifically cautious, behaviorally anchored step toward reliable, epistemically vigilant single-agent orchestration.

</details>


### [112] [Machine individuality: Separating genuine idiosyncrasy from response bias in large language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16755)
*Valentin Kriegmair,Dirk U. Wulff*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究使用交叉随机效应模型分析10个开源大语言模型对10万个单词在14个心理语言学规范上的评分，发现16.9%的方差可归因于刺激特异性个体差异，这种"机器个体性"是稳定且独特的模型特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型在日常生活中的应用日益广泛，理解它们的行为倾向变得至关重要。现有研究使用心理测量量表和认知范式来分析LLM的倾向，但无法区分行为差异是源于稳定的、刺激特异性的个体差异，还是全局响应偏差和随机噪声。

Method: 采用交叉随机效应模型（心理测量学中用于分离系统效应的常用方法），分析10个开源权重LLM对超过10万个单词在14个心理语言学规范上提供的7490万个评分数据。

Result: 平均16.9%的方差可归因于刺激特异性个体差异，显著超过统计零模型。跨规范预测分析显示这种个体性构成了每个模型独特的连贯指纹，这些差异不能归因于响应偏差或随机噪声。

Conclusion: 研究识别出大语言模型中存在的个体差异，这些差异反映了稳定的、刺激特异性的"机器个体性"，为理解LLM行为倾向提供了新的方法论和概念框架。

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) are increasingly integrated into daily life, in roles ranging from high-stakes decision support to companionship, understanding their behavioral dispositions becomes critical. A growing literature uses psychometric inventories and cognitive paradigms to profile LLM dispositions. However, these approaches cannot determine whether behavioral differences reflect stable, stimulus-specific individuality or global response biases and stochastic noise. Here, we apply crossed random-effects models -- widely used in psychometrics to separate systematic effects -- to 74.9 million ratings provided by 10 open-weight LLMs for over 100,000 words across 14 psycholinguistic norms. On average, 16.9% of variance is attributable to stimulus-specific individuality, robustly exceeding a statistical null model. Cross-norm prediction analyses reveal this individuality as a coherent fingerprint, unique to each model. These results identify individual differences among LLMs that cannot be attributed to response biases or stochastic noise. We term these differences machine individuality.

</details>


### [113] [SAVE: A Generalizable Framework for Multi-Condition Single-Cell Generation with Gene Block Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16776)
*Jiahao Li,Jiayi Dong,Peng Ye,Xiaochi Zhou,Haohai Lu,Fei Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SAVE是一个基于条件Transformer的统一生成框架，通过将语义相关基因分组为块来捕获基因模块间的高阶依赖关系，用于多条件单细胞建模，在生成保真度和外推泛化方面优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常将基因视为独立标记，忽略了它们的高层次生物学关系，导致性能不佳。需要一种能够跨多样生物和技术条件建模单细胞基因表达的方法，以表征细胞状态并模拟未见场景。

Method: SAVE采用条件Transformer框架，通过将语义相关基因分组为块来创建粗粒度表示，捕获基因模块间的高阶依赖关系。结合流匹配机制和条件掩码策略，增强灵活模拟能力并支持未见条件组合的泛化。

Result: SAVE在条件生成、批次效应校正和扰动预测等一系列基准测试中均优于现有最先进方法，在生成保真度和外推泛化方面表现突出，特别是在低资源或组合保留设置下。

Conclusion: SAVE为复杂单细胞数据建模提供了一个可扩展且可泛化的解决方案，在虚拟细胞合成和生物学解释方面具有广泛实用性。

Abstract: Modeling single-cell gene expression across diverse biological and technical conditions is crucial for characterizing cellular states and simulating unseen scenarios. Existing methods often treat genes as independent tokens, overlooking their high-level biological relationships and leading to poor performance. We introduce SAVE, a unified generative framework based on conditional Transformers for multi-condition single-cell modeling. SAVE leverages a coarse-grained representation by grouping semantically related genes into blocks, capturing higher-order dependencies among gene modules. A Flow Matching mechanism and condition-masking strategy further enhance flexible simulation and enable generalization to unseen condition combinations.
